Psalm 34:21	But malice shall slay the wicked: and they that hate the righteous, shall perish.
Psalm 34:22	The Lord redeemeth the soules of his seruants: and none, that trust in him, shall perish.
Psalm 35:1	A Psalme of Dauid. Pleade thou my cause, O Lord, with them that striue with me: fight thou against them, that fight against me.
Psalm 35:2	Lay hand vpon the shielde and buckler, and stand vp for mine helpe.
Psalm 35:3	Bring out also the speare and stop the way against them, that persecute me: say vnto my soule, I am thy saluation.
Psalm 35:4	Let them be confounded and put to shame, that seeke after my soule: let them be turned backe, and brought to confusion, that imagine mine hurt.
Psalm 35:5	Let them be as chaffe before the winde, and let the Angel of the Lord scatter them.
Psalm 35:6	Let their way be darke and slipperie: and let the Angel of the Lord persecute them.
Psalm 35:7	For without cause they haue hid the pit and their net for me: without cause haue they digged a pit for my soule.
Psalm 35:8	Let destruction come vpon him at vnwares, and let his net, that he hath laid priuilie, take him: let him fall into the same destruction.
Psalm 35:9	Then my soule shalbe ioyfull in the Lord: it shall reioyce in his saluation.
Psalm 35:10	All my bones shall say, Lord, who is like vnto thee, which deliuerest the poore from him, that is too strong for him! yea, the poore and him that is in miserie, from him that spoyleth him!
Psalm 35:11	Cruell witnesses did rise vp: they asked of me things that I knewe not.
Psalm 35:12	They rewarded me euill for good, to haue spoyled my soule.
Psalm 35:13	Yet I, when they were sicke, I was clothed with a sacke: I humbled my soule with fasting: and my praier was turned vpon my bosome.
Psalm 35:14	I behaued my selfe as to my friend, or as to my brother: I humbled my selfe, mourning as one that bewaileth his mother.
Psalm 35:15	But in mine aduersitie they reioyced, and gathered them selues together: the abiects assembled themselues against me, and knewe not: they tare me and ceased not,
Psalm 35:16	With the false skoffers at bankets, gnashing their teeth against me.
Psalm 35:17	Lord, how long wilt thou beholde this? deliuer my soule from their tumult, euen my desolate soule from the lions.
Psalm 35:18	So will I giue thee thankes in a great Congregation: I will praise thee among much people.
Psalm 35:19	Let not them that are mine enemies, vniustly reioyce ouer mee, neyther let them winke with the eye, that hate mee without a cause.
Psalm 35:20	For they speake not as friendes: but they imagine deceitfull woordes against the quiet of the lande.
Psalm 35:21	And they gaped on mee with their mouthes, saying, Aha, aha, our eye hath seene.
Psalm 35:22	Thou hast seene it, O Lord: keepe not silence: be not farre from me, O Lord.
Psalm 35:23	Arise and wake to my iudgement, euen to my cause, my God, and my Lord.
Psalm 35:24	Iudge me, O Lord my God, according to thy righteousnesse, and let them not reioyce ouer mee.
Psalm 35:25	Let them not say in their hearts, O our soule reioyce: neither let them say, We haue deuoured him.
Psalm 35:26	Let them bee confounded, and put to shame together, that reioyce at mine hurt: let them bee clothed with confusion and shame, that lift vp themselues against me.
Psalm 35:27	But let them be ioyful and glad, that loue my righteousnesse: yea, let them say alway, Let the Lord be magnified, which loueth the prosperitie of his seruant.
Psalm 35:28	And my tongue shall vtter thy righteousnesse, and thy praise euery day.
Psalm 36:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of Dauid, the servant of the Lord. Wickedness sayeth to the wicked man, euen in mine heart, that there is no feare of God before his eyes.
Psalm 36:2	For hee flattereth himselfe in his owne eyes, while his iniquitie is foud worthy to be hated.
Psalm 36:3	The wordes of his mouth are iniquitie and deceit: hee hath left off to vnderstand and to doe good.
Psalm 36:4	Hee imagineth mischiefe vpon his bed: he setteth himselfe vpon a way, that is not good, and doeth not abhorre euill.
Psalm 36:5	Thy mercy, O Lord, reacheth vnto the heauens, and thy faithfulnesse vnto the cloudes.
Psalm 36:6	Thy righteousnesse is like the mightie moutaines: thy iudgements are like a great deepe: thou, Lord, doest saue man and beast.
Psalm 36:7	How excellent is thy mercy, O God! therefore the children of men trust vnder the shadowe of thy wings.
Psalm 36:8	They shall be satisfied with the fatnesse of thine house, and thou shalt giue them drinke out of the riuer of thy pleasures.
Psalm 36:9	For with thee is the well of life, and in thy light shall we see light.
Psalm 36:10	Extend thy louing kindnes vnto them that knowe thee, and thy righteousnesse vnto them that are vpright in heart.
Psalm 36:11	Let not ye foote of pride come against me, and let not the hand of ye wicked men moue me.
Psalm 36:12	There they are fallen that worke iniquity: they are cast downe, and shall not be able to rise.
Psalm 37:1	A Psalme of David. Fret not thy selfe because of the wicked men, neither be enuious for the euill doers.
Psalm 37:2	For they shall soone bee cut downe like grasse, and shall wither as the greene herbe.
Psalm 37:3	Trust thou in the Lord and do good: dwell in the land, and thou shalt be fed assuredly.
Psalm 37:4	And delite thy selfe in the Lord, and hee shall giue thee thine hearts desire.
Psalm 37:5	Commit thy way vnto the Lord, and trust in him, and he shall bring it to passe.
Psalm 37:6	And he shall bring foorth thy righteousnes as the light, and thy iudgement as the noone day.
Psalm 37:7	Waite patiently vpon the Lord and hope in him: fret not thy selfe for him which prospereth in his way: nor for the man that bringeth his enterprises to passe.
Psalm 37:8	Cease from anger, and leaue off wrath: fret not thy selfe also to doe euill.
Psalm 37:9	For euill doers shalbe cut off, and they that wait vpon the Lord, they shall inherite the land.
Psalm 37:10	Therefore yet a litle while, and the wicked shall not appeare, and thou shalt looke after his place, and he shall not be found.
Psalm 37:11	But meeke men shall possesse the earth, and shall haue their delite in the multitude of peace.
Psalm 37:12	The wicked practiseth against the iust, and gnasheth his teeth against him.
Psalm 37:13	But the Lord shall laugh him to scorne: for he seeth, that his day is comming.
Psalm 37:14	The wicked haue drawen their sworde, and haue bent their bowe, to cast downe the poore and needie, and to slay such as be of vpright conuersation.
Psalm 37:15	But their sword shall enter into their owne heart, and their bowes shalbe broken.
Psalm 37:16	A small thing vnto the iust man is better, then great riches to the wicked and mightie.
Psalm 37:17	For the armes of the wicked shall be broken: but the Lord vpholdeth the iust men.
Psalm 37:18	The Lord knoweth the dayes of vpright men, and their inheritance shall bee perpetuall.
Psalm 37:19	They shall not be confounded in the perilous time, and in the daies of famine they shall haue ynough.
Psalm 37:20	But the wicked shall perish, and the enemies of the Lord shall be consumed as the fatte of lambes: euen with the smoke shall they consume away.
Psalm 37:21	The wicked boroweth and payeth not againe. but the righteous is mercifull, and giueth.
Psalm 37:22	For such as be blessed of God, shall inherite the lande, and they that be cursed of him, shalbe cut off.
Psalm 37:23	The pathes of man are directed by the Lord: for he loueth his way.
Psalm 37:24	Though he fall, hee shall not be cast off: for the Lord putteth vnder his hand.
Psalm 37:25	I haue beene yong, and am olde: yet I sawe neuer the righteous forsaken, nor his seede begging bread.
Psalm 37:26	But hee is euer mercifull and lendeth, and his seede enioyeth the blessing.
Psalm 37:27	Flee from euill and doe good, and dwell for euer.
Psalm 37:28	For the Lord loueth iudgement, and forsaketh not his Saintes: they shall be preserued for euermore: but the seede of the wicked shall be cut off.
Psalm 37:29	The righteous men shall inherit the lande, and dwell therein for euer.
Psalm 37:30	The mouth of the righteous will speake of wisedome, and his tongue will talke of iudgement.
Psalm 37:31	For the Lawe of his God is in his heart, and his steppes shall not slide.
Psalm 37:32	The wicked watcheth the righteous, and seeketh to slay him.
Psalm 37:33	But the Lord wil not leaue him in his hand, nor condemne him, when he is iudged.
Psalm 37:34	Waite thou on the Lord, and keepe his way, and he shall exalt thee, that thou shalt inherite the lande: when the wicked men shall perish, thou shalt see.
Psalm 37:35	I haue seene the wicked strong, and spreading himselfe like a greene bay tree.
Psalm 37:36	Yet he passed away, and loe, he was gone, and I sought him, but he could not be founde.
Psalm 37:37	Marke the vpright man, and beholde the iust: for the end of that man is peace.
Psalm 37:38	But the transgressours shall be destroyed together, and the ende of the wicked shall bee cut off.
Psalm 37:39	But the saluation of the righteous men shalbe of the Lord: he shalbe their strength in the time of trouble.
Psalm 37:40	For the Lord shall helpe them, and deliuer them: he shall deliuer them from the wicked, and shall saue them, because they trust in him.
Psalm 38:1	A Psalme of Dauid for remembrance. O Lord, rebuke mee not in thine anger, neither chastise me in thy wrath.
Psalm 38:2	For thine arrowes haue light vpon me, and thine hand lyeth vpon me.
Psalm 38:3	There is nothing sound in my flesh, because of thine anger: neither is there rest in my bones because of my sinne.
Psalm 38:4	For mine iniquities are gone ouer mine head, and as a weightie burden they are too heauie for me.
Psalm 38:5	My woundes are putrified, and corrupt because of my foolishnes.
Psalm 38:6	I am bowed, and crooked very sore: I goe mourning all the day.
Psalm 38:7	For my reines are full of burning, and there is nothing sound in my flesh.
Psalm 38:8	I am weakened and sore broken: I roare for the very griefe of mine heart.
Psalm 38:9	Lord, I powre my whole desire before thee, and my sighing is not hid from thee.
Psalm 38:10	Mine heart panteth: my strength faileth me, and the light of mine eyes, euen they are not mine owne.
Psalm 38:11	My louers and my friends stand aside from my plague, and my kinsmen stand a farre off.
Psalm 38:12	They also, that seeke after my life, laye snares, and they that go about to do me euil, talke wicked things and imagine deceite continually.
Psalm 38:13	But I as a deafe man heard not, and am as a dumme man, which openeth not his mouth.
Psalm 38:14	Thus am I as a man, that heareth not, and in whose mouth are no reproofes.
Psalm 38:15	For on thee, O Lord, do I waite: thou wilt heare me, my Lord, my God.
Psalm 38:16	For I said, Heare me, least they reioyce ouer me: for when my foote slippeth, they extol themselues against me.
Psalm 38:17	Surely I am ready to halte, and my sorow is euer before me.
Psalm 38:18	When I declare my paine, and am sory for my sinne,
Psalm 38:19	Then mine enemies are aliue and are mightie, and they that hate me wrongfully are many.
Psalm 38:20	They also, that rewarde euill for good, are mine aduersaries, because I follow goodnesse.
Psalm 38:21	Forsake me not, O Lord: be not thou farre from me, my God.
Psalm 38:22	Haste thee to helpe mee, O my Lord, my saluation.
Psalm 39:1	To the excellent musician Ieduthun. I thought, I will take heede to my wayes, that I sinne not with my tongue: I will keepe my mouth brideled, while the wicked is in my sight.
Psalm 39:2	I was dumme and spake nothing: I kept silece euen from good, and my sorow was more stirred.
Psalm 39:3	Mine heart was hote within me, and while I was musing, the fire kindeled, and I spake with my tongue, saying,
Psalm 39:4	Lord, let me know mine ende, and the measure of my dayes, what it is: let mee knowe howe long I haue to liue.
Psalm 39:5	Beholde, thou hast made my dayes as an hand breadth, and mine age as nothing in respect of thee: surely euery man in his best state is altogether vanitie. Selah.
Psalm 39:6	Doubtlesse man walketh in a shadowe, and disquieteth himselfe in vaine: he heapeth vp riches, and cannot tell who shall gather them.
Psalm 39:7	And now Lord, what wait I for? mine hope is euen in thee.
Psalm 39:8	Deliuer me from all my transgressions, and make me not a rebuke vnto the foolish.
Psalm 39:9	I should haue bene dumme, and not haue opened my mouth, because thou didest it.
Psalm 39:10	Take thy plague away from mee: for I am consumed by the stroke of thine hand.
Psalm 39:11	When thou with rebukes doest chastise man for iniquitie, thou as a mothe makest his beautie to consume: surely euery man is vanitie. Selah.
Psalm 39:12	Heare my prayer, O Lord, and hearken vnto my cry: keepe not silence at my teares, for I am a strager with thee, and a soiourner as all my fathers.
Psalm 39:13	Stay thine anger from me, that I may recouer my strength, before I go hence and be not.
Psalm 40:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of David. I Waited paciently for the Lord, and he inclined vnto me, and heard my cry.
Psalm 40:2	Hee brought mee also out of the horrible pit, out of the myrie clay, and set my feete vpon the rocke, and ordered my goings.
Psalm 40:3	And he hath put in my mouth a new song of praise vnto our God: many shall see it and feare, and shall trust in the Lord.
Psalm 40:4	Blessed is the man that maketh the Lord his trust, and regardeth not the proude, nor such as turne aside to lyes.
Psalm 40:5	O Lord my God, thou hast made thy wonderfull workes so many, that none can count in order to thee thy thoughts toward vs: I would declare, and speake of them, but they are moe then I am able to expresse.
Psalm 40:6	Sacrifice and offering thou didest not desire: (for mine eares hast thou prepared) burnt offring and sinne offering hast thou not required.
Psalm 40:7	Then said I, Lo, I come: for in the rolle of the booke it is written of me,
Psalm 40:8	I desired to doe thy good will, O my God: yea, thy Lawe is within mine heart.
Psalm 40:9	I haue declared thy righteousnesse in the great Congregation: loe, I will not refraine my lippes: O Lord, thou knowest.
Psalm 40:10	I haue not hidde thy righteousnesse within mine heart, but I haue declared thy trueth and thy saluation: I haue not conceiled thy mercy and thy trueth from the great Congregation.
Psalm 40:11	Withdrawe not thou thy tender mercie from mee, O Lord: let thy mercie and thy trueth alway preserue me.
Psalm 40:12	For innumerable troubles haue compassed mee: my sinnes haue taken such holde vpon me, that I am not able to looke vp: yea, they are moe in nomber then the heares of mine head: therefore mine heart hath failed me.
Psalm 40:13	Let it please thee, O Lord, to deliuer mee: make haste, O Lord, to helpe me.
Psalm 40:14	Let them be confounded and put to shame together, that seeke my soule to destroye it: let them be driuen backward and put to rebuke, that desire mine hurt.
Psalm 40:15	Let them be destroyed for a rewarde of their shame, which say vnto me, Aha, aha.
Psalm 40:16	Let all them, that seeke thee, reioyce and be glad in thee: and let them, that loue thy saluation, say alway, The Lord be praysed.
Psalm 40:17	Though I be poore and needie, the Lord thinketh on mee: thou art mine helper and my deliuerer: my God, make no tarying.
Psalm 41:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of Dauid. Blessed is he that iudgeth wisely of the poore: the Lord shall deliuer him in ye time of trouble.
Psalm 41:2	The Lord will keepe him, and preserue him aliue: he shalbe blessed vpon the earth, and thou wilt not deliuer him vnto the will of his enemies.
Psalm 41:3	The Lord wil strengthen him vpon ye bed of sorow: thou hast turned al his bed in his sicknes.
Psalm 41:4	Therefore I saide, Lord haue mercie vpon me: heale my soule, for I haue sinned against thee.
Psalm 41:5	Mine enemies speake euill of me, saying, When shall he die, and his name perish?
Psalm 41:6	And if hee come to see mee, hee speaketh lies, but his heart heapeth iniquitie within him, and when he commeth foorth, he telleth it.
Psalm 41:7	All they that hate me, whisper together against me: euen against me do they imagine mine hurt.
Psalm 41:8	A mischiefe is light vpon him, and he that lyeth, shall no more rise.
Psalm 41:9	Yea, my familiar friend, whom I trusted, which did eate of my bread, hath lifted vp the heele against me.
Psalm 41:10	Therefore, O Lord, haue mercy vpon mee, and raise me vp: so I shall reward them.
Psalm 41:11	By this I know that thou fauourest me, because mine enemie doth not triumph against me.
Psalm 41:12	And as for me, thou vpholdest me in mine integritie, and doest set me before thy face for euer.
Psalm 41:13	Blessed be the Lord God of Israel worlde without ende. So be it, euen so be it.
Psalm 42:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme to give instruction, committed to the sonnes of Korah. As the harte brayeth for the riuers of water, so panteth my soule after thee, O God.
Psalm 42:2	My soule thirsteth for God, euen for the liuing God: when shall I come and appeare before the presence of God?
Psalm 42:3	My teares haue bin my meate day and night, while they dayly say vnto me, Where is thy God?
Psalm 42:4	When I remembred these things, I powred out my very heart, because I had gone with the multitude, and ledde them into the House of God with the voyce of singing, and prayse, as a multitude that keepeth a feast.
Psalm 42:5	Why art thou cast downe, my soule, and vnquiet within me? waite on God: for I will yet giue him thankes for the helpe of his presence.
Psalm 42:6	My God, my soule is cast downe within me, because I remember thee, from the land of Iorden, and Hermonim, and from the mount Mizar.
Psalm 42:7	One deepe calleth another deepe by the noyse of thy water spoutes: all thy waues and thy floods are gone ouer me.
Psalm 42:8	The Lord will graunt his louing kindenesse in the day, and in the night shall I sing of him, euen a prayer vnto the God of my life.
Psalm 42:9	I wil say vnto God, which is my rocke, Why hast thou forgotten mee? why goe I mourning, when the enemie oppresseth me?
Psalm 42:10	My bones are cut asunder, while mine enemies reproch me, saying dayly vnto me, Where is thy God?
Psalm 42:11	Why art thou cast downe, my soule? and why art thou disquieted within mee? waite on God: for I wil yet giue him thankes: he is my present helpe, and my God.
Psalm 43:1	Judge me, O God, and defend my cause against the vnmercifull people: deliuer mee from the deceitfull and wicked man.
Psalm 43:2	For thou art the God of my strength: why hast thou put me away? why goe I so mourning, when the enemie oppresseth me?
Psalm 43:3	Sende thy light and thy trueth: let them leade mee: let them bring mee vnto thine holy Mountaine and to thy Tabernacles.
Psalm 43:4	Then wil I go vnto the altar of God, euen vnto the God of my ioy and gladnes: and vpon the harpe wil I giue thanks vnto thee, O God, my God.
Psalm 43:5	Why art thou cast downe, my soule? and why art thou disquieted within mee? waite on God: for I will yet giue him thankes, he is my present helpe, and my God.
Psalm 44:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme to give instruction, committed to the sonnes of Korah. We haue heard with our eares, O God: our fathers haue tolde vs the workes, that thou hast done in their dayes, in the olde time:
Psalm 44:2	Howe thou hast driuen out the heathen with thine hand, and planted them: how thou hast destroyed the people, and caused them to grow.
Psalm 44:3	For they inherited not the lande by their owne sworde, neither did their owne arme saue them: but thy right hand, and thine arme and the light of thy countenance, because thou didest fauour them.
Psalm 44:4	Thou art my King, O God: send helpe vnto Iaakob.
Psalm 44:5	Through thee haue we thrust backe our aduersaries: by thy Name haue we troden downe them that rose vp against vs.
Psalm 44:6	For I do not trust in my bowe, neither can my sworde saue me.
Psalm 44:7	But thou hast saued vs from our aduersaries, and hast put them to confusion that hate vs.
Psalm 44:8	Therefore will wee praise God continually, and will confesse thy Name for euer. Selah.
Psalm 44:9	But now thou art farre off, and puttest vs to confusion, and goest not forth with our armies.
Psalm 44:10	Thou makest vs to turne backe from the aduersary, and they, which hate vs, spoile for theselues.
Psalm 44:11	Thou giuest vs as sheepe to bee eaten, and doest scatter vs among the nations.
Psalm 44:12	Thou sellest thy people without gaine, and doest not increase their price.
Psalm 44:13	Thou makest vs a reproche to our neighbours, a iest and a laughing stocke to them that are round about vs.
Psalm 44:14	Thou makest vs a prouerbe among the nations, and a nodding of the head among the people.
Psalm 44:15	My confusion is dayly before me, and the shame of my face hath couered me,
Psalm 44:16	For the voyce of the slaunderer and rebuker, for the enemie and auenger.
Psalm 44:17	All this is come vpon vs, yet doe wee not forget thee, neither deale wee falsly concerning thy couenant.
Psalm 44:18	Our heart is not turned backe: neither our steps gone out of thy paths,
Psalm 44:19	Albeit thou hast smitten vs downe into the place of dragons, and couered vs with the shadow of death.
Psalm 44:20	If wee haue forgotten the Name of our God, and holden vp our hands to a strange god,
Psalm 44:21	Shall not God searche this out? for hee knoweth the secrets of the heart.
Psalm 44:22	Surely for thy sake are we slaine continually, and are counted as sheepe for the slaughter.
Psalm 44:23	Vp, why sleepest thou, O Lord? awake, be not farre off for euer.
Psalm 44:24	Wherefore hidest thou thy face? and forgettest our miserie and our affliction?
Psalm 44:25	For our soule is beaten downe vnto the dust: our belly cleaueth vnto the ground.
Psalm 44:26	Rise vp for our succour, and redeeme vs for thy mercies sake.
Psalm 45:1	To him that excelleth on Shoshannim a song of love to give instruction, committed to the sonnes of Korah. Mine heart will vtter forth a good matter: I wil intreat in my workes of the King: my tongue is as the pen of a swift writer.
Psalm 45:2	Thou art fayrer then the children of men: grace is powred in thy lips, because God hath blessed thee for euer.
Psalm 45:3	Gird thy sword vpon thy thigh, O most mightie, to wit, thy worship and thy glory,
Psalm 45:4	And prosper with thy glory: ride vpon the worde of trueth and of meekenes and of righteousnes: so thy right hand shall teach thee terrible things.
Psalm 45:5	Thine arrowes are sharpe to pearce the heart of the Kings enemies: therefore the people shall fall vnder thee.
Psalm 45:6	Thy throne, O God, is for euer and euer: the scepter of thy kingdome is a scepter of righteousnesse.
Psalm 45:7	Thou louest righteousnes, and hatest wickednesse, because God, euen thy God hath anoynted thee with the oyle of gladnes aboue thy fellowes.
Psalm 45:8	All thy garments smelll of myrrhe and aloes, and cassia, when thou commest out of the yuorie palaces, where they haue made thee glad.
Psalm 45:9	Kings daugthers were among thine honorable wiues: vpon thy right hand did stand the Queene in a vesture of golde of Ophir.
Psalm 45:10	Hearken, O daughter, and consider, and incline thine eare: forget also thine owne people and thy fathers house.
Psalm 45:11	So shall the King haue pleasure in thy beautie: for he is thy Lord, and reuerence thou him.
Psalm 45:12	And the daughter of Tyrus with the rich of the people shall doe homage before thy face with presents.
Psalm 45:13	The Kings daughter is all glorious within: her clothing is of broydred golde.
Psalm 45:14	She shalbe brought vnto the King in raiment of needle worke: the virgins that follow after her, and her companions shall be brought vnto thee.
Psalm 45:15	With ioy and gladnes shall they be brought, and shall enter into the Kings palace.
Psalm 45:16	In steade of thy fathers shall thy children be: thou shalt make them princes through all the earth.
Psalm 45:17	I will make thy Name to be remembred through all generations: therefore shall the people giue thanks vnto thee world without ende.
Psalm 46:1	To him that excelleth upon Alamoth a song committed to the sonnes of Korah. God is our hope and strength, and helpe in troubles, ready to be found.
Psalm 46:2	Therefore will not we feare, though the earth be moued, and though the mountaines fall into the middes of the sea.
Psalm 46:3	Though the waters thereof rage and be troubled, and the mountaines shake at the surges of the same. Selah,
Psalm 46:4	Yet there is a Riuer, whose streames shall make glad the citie of God: euen the Sanctuarie of the Tabernacles of the most High.
Psalm 46:5	God is in the middes of it: therefore shall it not be moued: God shall helpe it very earely.
Psalm 46:6	When the nations raged, and the kingdomes were moued, God thundred, and the earth melted.
Psalm 46:7	The Lord of hostes is with vs: the God of Iaakob is our refuge. Selah.
Psalm 46:8	Come, and behold the workes of the Lord, what desolations he hath made in the earth.
Psalm 46:9	He maketh warres to cease vnto the endes of the world: he breaketh the bowe and cutteth the speare, and burneth the chariots with fire.
Psalm 46:10	Be still and knowe that I am God: I will be exalted among the heathen, and I wil be exalted in the earth.
Psalm 46:11	The Lord of hostes is with vs: the God of Iaakob is our refuge. Selah.
Psalm 47:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme committed to the sonnes of Korah. All people clap your hands: sing loude vnto God with a ioyfull voyce.
Psalm 47:2	For the Lord is high, and terrible: a great King ouer all the earth.
Psalm 47:3	He hath subdued the people vnder vs, and the nations vnder our feete.
Psalm 47:4	Hee hath chosen our inheritance for vs: euen the glory of Iaakob whom he loued. Selah.
Psalm 47:5	God is gone vp with triumph, euen the Lord, with the sound of the trumpet.
Psalm 47:6	Sing prayses to God, sing prayses: sing prayses vnto our King, sing prayses.
Psalm 47:7	For God is the King of all the earth: sing prayses euery one that hath vnderstanding.
Psalm 47:8	God reigneth ouer the heathen: God sitteth vpon his holy throne.
Psalm 47:9	The princes of the people are gathered vnto the people of the God of Abraham: for the shields of the world belong to God: he is greatly to be exalted.
Psalm 48:1	A song or Psalme committed to the sonnes of Korah. Great is the Lord, and greatly to be praysed, in the Citie of our God, euen vpon his holy Mountaine.
Psalm 48:2	Mount Zion, lying Northwarde, is faire in situation: it is the ioy of the whole earth, and the Citie of the great King.
Psalm 48:3	In the palaces thereof God is knowen for a refuge.
Psalm 48:4	For lo, the Kings were gathered, and went together.
Psalm 48:5	When they sawe it, they marueiled: they were astonied, and suddenly driuen backe.
Psalm 48:6	Feare came there vpon them, and sorowe, as vpon a woman in trauaile.
Psalm 48:7	As with an East winde thou breakest the shippes of Tarshish, so were they destroyed.
Psalm 48:8	As we haue heard, so haue we seene in the citie of the Lord of hostes, in the Citie of our God: God will stablish it for euer. Selah.
Psalm 48:9	We waite for thy louing kindnes, O God, in the middes of thy Temple.
Psalm 48:10	O God, according vnto thy Name, so is thy prayse vnto the worlds end: thy right hand is full of righteousnes.
Psalm 48:11	Let mount Zion reioyce, and the daughters of Iudah be glad, because of thy iudgements.
Psalm 48:12	Compasse about Zion, and goe round about it, and tell the towres thereof.
Psalm 48:13	Marke well the wall thereof: beholde her towres, that ye may tell your posteritie.
Psalm 48:14	For this God is our God for euer and euer: he shall be our guide vnto the death.
Psalm 49:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme committed to the sonnes of Korah. Heare this, all ye people: giue eare, all ye that dwell in the world,
Psalm 49:2	As well lowe as hie, both rich and poore.
Psalm 49:3	My mouth shall speake of wisdome, and the meditation of mine heart is of knowledge.
Psalm 49:4	I will incline mine eare to a parable, and vtter my graue matter vpon the harpe.
Psalm 49:5	Wherefore should I feare in the euil dayes, when iniquitie shall compasse me about, as at mine heeles?
Psalm 49:6	They trust in their goods, and boast them selues in the multitude of their riches.
Psalm 49:7	Yet a man can by no meanes redeeme his brother: he can not giue his raunsome to God,
Psalm 49:8	(So precious is the redemption of their soules, and the continuance for euer)
Psalm 49:9	That he may liue still for euer, and not see the graue.
Psalm 49:10	For he seeth that wise men die, and also that the ignorant and foolish perish, and leaue their riches for others.
Psalm 49:11	Yet they thinke, their houses, and their habitations shall continue for euer, euen from generation to generation, and call their lands by their names.
Psalm 49:12	But man shall not continue in honour: he is like the beastes that die.
Psalm 49:13	This their way vttereth their foolishnes: yet their posteritie delite in their talke. Selah.
Psalm 49:14	Like sheepe they lie in graue: death deuoureth them, and the righteous shall haue domination ouer them in the morning: for their beautie shall consume, when they shall goe from their house to graue.
Psalm 49:15	But God shall deliuer my soule from the power of the graue: for he will receiue me. Selah.
Psalm 49:16	Be not thou afrayd when one is made rich, and when the glory of his house is increased.
Psalm 49:17	For he shall take nothing away when he dieth, neither shall his pompe descende after him.
Psalm 49:18	For while he liued, he reioyced himselfe: and men will prayse thee, when thou makest much of thy selfe.
Psalm 49:19	He shall enter into the generation of his fathers, and they shall not liue for euer.
Psalm 49:20	Man is in honour, and vnderstandeth not: he is like to beasts that perish.
Psalm 50:1	A Psalme of Asaph. The God of Gods, euen the Lord hath spoken and called the earth from the rising vp of the sunne vnto the going downe thereof.
Psalm 50:2	Out of Zion, which is the perfection of beautie, hath God shined.
Psalm 50:3	Our God shall come and shall not keepe silence: a fire shall deuoure before him, and a mightie tempest shall be mooued round about him.
Psalm 50:4	Hee shall call the heauen aboue, and the earth to iudge his people.
Psalm 50:5	Gather my Saints together vnto me, those that make a couenant with me with sacrifice.
Psalm 50:6	And the heauens shall declare his righteousnes: for God is iudge himselfe. Selah.
Psalm 50:7	Heare, O my people, and I wil speake: heare, O Israel, and I wil testifie vnto thee: for I am God, euen thy God.
Psalm 50:8	I wil not reproue thee for thy sacrifices, or thy burnt offerings, that haue not bene continually before me.
Psalm 50:9	I will take no bullocke out of thine house, nor goates out of thy foldes.
Psalm 50:10	For all the beastes of the forest are mine, and the beastes on a thousand mountaines.
Psalm 50:11	I knowe all the foules on the mountaines: and the wilde beastes of the fielde are mine.
Psalm 50:12	If I bee hungry, I will not tell thee: for the world is mine, and all that therein is.
Psalm 50:13	Will I eate the flesh of bulles? or drinke the blood of goates?
Psalm 50:14	Offer vnto God praise, and pay thy vowes vnto the most High,
Psalm 50:15	And call vpon me in the day of trouble: so will I deliuer thee, and thou shalt glorifie me.
Psalm 50:16	But vnto the wicked said God, What hast thou to doe to declare mine ordinances, that thou shouldest take my couenant in thy mouth,
Psalm 50:17	Seeing thou hatest to bee reformed, and hast cast my wordes behinde thee?
Psalm 50:18	For when thou seest a thiefe, thou runnest with him, and thou art partaker with the adulterers.
Psalm 50:19	Thou giuest thy mouth to euill, and with thy tongue thou forgest deceit.
Psalm 50:20	Thou sittest, and speakest against thy brother, and slanderest thy mothers sonne.
Psalm 50:21	These things hast thou done, and I held my tongue: therefore thou thoughtest that I was like thee: but I will reproue thee, and set them in order before thee.
Psalm 50:22	Oh cosider this, ye that forget God, least I teare you in pieces, and there be none that can deliuer you.
Psalm 50:23	He that offereth praise, shall glorifie mee: and to him, that disposeth his way aright, will I shew the saluation of God.
Psalm 51:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of David, when the Prophet Nathan came unto him, after he had gone in to Bath-sheba. Have mercie vpon me, O God, according to thy louing kindnes: according to the multitude of thy compassions put away mine iniquities.
Psalm 51:2	Wash me throughly from mine iniquitie, and clense me from my sinne.
Psalm 51:3	For I know mine iniquities, and my sinne is euer before me.
Psalm 51:4	Against thee, against thee onely haue I sinned, and done euill in thy sight, that thou mayest be iust when thou speakest, and pure when thou iudgest.
Psalm 51:5	Beholde, I was borne in iniquitie, and in sinne hath my mother conceiued me.
Psalm 51:6	Beholde, thou louest trueth in the inwarde affections: therefore hast thou taught mee wisedome in the secret of mine heart.
Psalm 51:7	Purge me with hyssope, and I shalbe cleane: wash me, and I shalbe whiter then snowe.
Psalm 51:8	Make me to heare ioye and gladnes, that the bones, which thou hast broken, may reioyce.
Psalm 51:9	Hide thy face from my sinnes, and put away all mine iniquities.
Psalm 51:10	Create in mee a cleane heart, O God, and renue a right spirit within me.
Psalm 51:11	Cast mee not away from thy presence, and take not thine holy Spirit from me.
Psalm 51:12	Restore to me the ioy of thy saluation, and stablish me with thy free Spirit.
Psalm 51:13	Then shall I teache thy wayes vnto the wicked, and sinners shalbe conuerted vnto thee.
Psalm 51:14	Deliuer me from blood, O God, which art the God of my saluation, and my tongue shall sing ioyfully of thy righteousnes.
Psalm 51:15	Open thou my lippes, O Lord, and my mouth shall shewe foorth thy praise.
Psalm 51:16	For thou desirest no sacrifice, though I would giue it: thou delitest not in burnt offering.
Psalm 51:17	The sacrifices of God are a contrite spirit: a contrite and a broken heart, O God, thou wilt not despise.
Psalm 51:18	Bee fauourable vnto Zion for thy good pleasure: builde the walles of Ierusalem.
Psalm 51:19	Then shalt thou accept ye sacrifices of righteousnes, euen the burnt offering and oblation: then shall they offer calues vpon thine altar.
Psalm 52:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of Dauid to giue instruction. When Doeg the Edomite came and shewed Saul, and saide to him, Dauid is come to the house of Abimelech. Why boastest thou thy selfe in thy wickednesse, O man of power? the louing kindenesse of God indureth dayly.
Psalm 52:2	Thy tongue imagineth mischiefe, and is like a sharpe rasor, that cutteth deceitfully.
Psalm 52:3	Thou doest loue euill more then good, and lies more then to speake the trueth. Selah.
Psalm 52:4	Thou louest all wordes that may destroye, O deceitfull tongue!
Psalm 52:5	So shall God destroy thee for euer: he shall take thee and plucke thee out of thy tabernacle, and roote thee out of ye land of the liuing. Selah.
Psalm 52:6	The righteous also shall see it, and feare, and shall laugh at him, saying,
Psalm 52:7	Beholde the man that tooke not God for his strength, but trusted vnto the multitude of his riches, and put his strength in his malice.
Psalm 52:8	But I shall bee like a greene oliue tree in the house of God: for I trusted in the mercie of God for euer and euer.
Psalm 52:9	I will alway praise thee, for that thou hast done this, and I will hope in thy Name, because it is good before thy Saints.
Psalm 53:1	To him that excelleth on Mahalath. A Psalme of David to give instruction. The foole hath saide in his heart, There is no God. they haue corrupted and done abominable wickednes: there is none that doeth good.
Psalm 53:2	God looked downe from heauen vpon the children of men, to see if there were any that would vnderstand, and seeke God.
Psalm 53:3	Euery one is gone backe: they are altogether corrupt: there is none that doth good, no not one.
Psalm 53:4	Doe not the workers of iniquitie knowe that they eate vp my people as they eate bread? they call not vpon God.
Psalm 53:5	There they were afraide for feare, where no feare was: for God hath scattered the bones of him that besieged thee: thou hast put them to confusion, because God hath cast them off.
Psalm 53:6	Oh giue saluation vnto Israel out of Zion: when God turneth the captiuitie of his people, then Iaakob shall reioyce, and Israel shalbe glad.
Psalm 54:1	To him that excelleth on Neginoth. A Psalme of David, to give instruction. When the Ziphims came and said unto Saul, Is not David hid among us? Save mee, O God, by thy Name, and by thy power iudge me.
Psalm 54:2	O God, heare my prayer: hearken vnto the wordes of my mouth.
Psalm 54:3	For strangers are risen vp against me, and tyrants seeke my soule: they haue not set God before them. Selah.
Psalm 54:4	Beholde, God is mine helper: the Lord is with them that vpholde my soule.
Psalm 54:5	He shall rewarde euill vnto mine enemies: Oh cut them off in thy trueth!
Psalm 54:6	Then I will sacrifice freely vnto thee: I wil praise thy Name, O Lord, because it is good.
Psalm 54:7	For he hath deliuered me out of al trouble, and mine eye hath seene my desire vpon mine enemies.
Psalm 55:1	To him that excelleth on Neginoth. A Psalme of David to give instruction. Heare my prayer, O God, and hide not thy selfe from my supplication.
Psalm 55:2	Hearken vnto me, and answere me: I mourne in my prayer, and make a noyse,
Psalm 55:3	For the voyce of the enemie, and for the vexation of ye wicked, because they haue brought iniquitie vpon me, and furiously hate me.
Psalm 55:4	Mine heart trembleth within mee, and the terrours of death are fallen vpon me.
Psalm 55:5	Feare and trembling are come vpon mee, and an horrible feare hath couered me.
Psalm 55:6	And I said, Oh that I had wings like a doue: then would I flie away and rest.
Psalm 55:7	Beholde, I woulde take my flight farre off, and lodge in the wildernes. Selah.
Psalm 55:8	Hee would make haste for my deliuerance from the stormie winde and tempest.
Psalm 55:9	Destroy, O Lord, and deuide their tongues: for I haue seene crueltie and strife in the citie.
Psalm 55:10	Day and night they goe about it vpon the walles thereof: both iniquitie and mischiefe are in the middes of it.
Psalm 55:11	Wickednes is in the middes thereof: deceit and guile depart not from her streetes.
Psalm 55:12	Surely mine enemie did not defame mee: for I could haue borne it: neither did mine aduersarie exalt himselfe against mee: for I would haue hid me from him.
Psalm 55:13	But it was thou, O man, euen my companion, my guide and my familiar:
Psalm 55:14	Which delited in consulting together, and went into the House of God as companions.
Psalm 55:15	Let death sense vpon them: let them goe downe quicke into the graue: for wickednes is in their dwellings, euen in the middes of them.
Psalm 55:16	But I will call vnto God, and the Lord will saue me.
Psalm 55:17	Euening and morning, and at noone will I pray, and make a noyse, and he wil heare my voice.
Psalm 55:18	He hath deliuered my soule in peace from the battel, that was against me: for many were with me.
Psalm 55:19	God shall heare and afflict them, euen hee that reigneth of olde, Selah. because they haue no changes, therefore they feare not God.
Psalm 55:20	Hee layed his hande vpon such, as be at peace with him, and he brake his couenant.
Psalm 55:21	The wordes of his mouth were softer then butter, yet warre was in his heart: his words were more gentle then oyle, yet they were swordes.
Psalm 55:22	Cast thy burden vpon the Lord, and hee shall nourish thee: he wil not suffer the righteous to fall for euer.
Psalm 55:23	And thou, O God, shalt bring them downe into the pitte of corruption: the bloudie, and deceitfull men shall not liue halfe their dayes: but I will trust in thee.
Psalm 56:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of David on Michtam, concerning the dumme doue in a farre countrey, when the Philistims tooke him in Gath. Be mercifull vnto me, O God, for man would swallow me vp: he fighteth continually and vexeth me.
Psalm 56:2	Mine enemies would dayly swallowe mee vp: for many fight against me, O thou most High.
Psalm 56:3	When I was afrayd, I trusted in thee.
Psalm 56:4	I will reioyce in God, because of his word, I trust in God, and will not feare what flesh can doe vnto me.
Psalm 56:5	Mine owne wordes grieue me dayly: all their thoughtes are against me to doe me hurt.
Psalm 56:6	They gather together, and keepe them selues close: they marke my steps, because they waite for my soule.
Psalm 56:7	They thinke they shall escape by iniquitie: O God, cast these people downe in thine anger.
Psalm 56:8	Thou hast counted my wandrings: put my teares into thy bottel: are they not in thy register?
Psalm 56:9	When I cry, then mine enemies shall turne backe: this I know, for God is with me.
Psalm 56:10	I will reioyce in God because of his worde: in the Lord wil I reioyce because of his worde.
Psalm 56:11	In God doe I trust: I will not be afrayd what man can doe vnto me.
Psalm 56:12	Thy vowes are vpon me, O God: I will render prayses vnto thee.
Psalm 56:13	For thou hast deliuered my soule from death, and also my feete from falling, that I may walke before God in the light of the liuing.
Psalm 57:1	To him that excelleth. Destroy not. A Psalme of David on Michtam. When he fled from Saul in the cave. Have mercie vpon me, O God, haue mercie vpon me: for my soule trusteth in thee, and in the shadowe of thy wings wil I trust, till these afflictions ouerpasse.
Psalm 57:2	I will call vnto the most high God, euen to the God, that performeth his promise toward me.
Psalm 57:3	He will send from heauen, and saue me from the reproofe of him that would swallowe me. Selah. God wil send his mercy, and his trueth.
Psalm 57:4	My soule is among lions: I lie among the children of men, that are set on fire: whose teeth are speares and arrowes, and their tongue a sharpe sworde.
Psalm 57:5	Exalt thy selfe, O God, aboue the heauen, and let thy glory be vpon all the earth.
Psalm 57:6	They haue layd a net for my steps: my soule is pressed downe: they haue digged a pit before me, and are fallen into the mids of it. Selah.
Psalm 57:7	Mine heart is prepared, O God, mine heart is prepared: I will sing and giue prayse.
Psalm 57:8	Awake my tongue, awake viole and harpe: I wil awake early.
Psalm 57:9	I will prayse thee, O Lord, among the people, and I wil sing vnto thee among the nations.
Psalm 57:10	For thy mercie is great vnto the heauens, and thy trueth vnto the cloudes.
Psalm 57:11	Exalt thy selfe, O God, aboue the heauens, and let thy glory be vpon all the earth.
Psalm 58:1	To him that excelleth. Destroy not. A Psalme of David on Michtam. Is it true? O Congregation, speake ye iustly? O sonnes of men, iudge ye vprightly?
Psalm 58:2	Yea, rather ye imagine mischiefe in your heart: your hands execute crueltie vpon the earth.
Psalm 58:3	The wicked are strangers from ye wombe: euen from the belly haue they erred, and speake lyes.
Psalm 58:4	Their poyson is euen like the poyson of a serpent: like ye deafe adder that stoppeth his eare.
Psalm 58:5	Which heareth not the voyce of the inchanter, though he be most expert in charming.
Psalm 58:6	Breake their teeth, O God, in their mouthes: breake the iawes of the yong lions, O Lord.
Psalm 58:7	Let them melt like the waters, let them passe away: when hee shooteth his arrowes, let them be as broken.
Psalm 58:8	Let them consume like a snayle that melteth, and like the vntimely fruite of a woman, that hath not seene the sunne.
Psalm 58:9	As raw flesh before your pots feele the fire of thornes: so let him cary them away as with a whirlewinde in his wrath.
Psalm 58:10	The righteous shall reioyce when he seeeth the vengeance: he shall wash his feete in the blood of the wicked.
Psalm 58:11	And men shall say, Verily there is fruite for the righteous: doutlesse there is a God that iudgeth in the earth.
Psalm 59:1	To him that excelleth. Destroy not. A Psalme of David on Michtam. When Saul sent and they did watch the house to kill him. O my God, deliuer mee from mine enemies: defend me from them that rise vp against me.
Psalm 59:2	Deliuer me from the wicked doers, and saue me from the bloody men.
Psalm 59:3	For loe, they haue layd waite for my soule: the mightie men are gathered against me, not for mine offence, nor for my sinne, O Lord.
Psalm 59:4	They runne and prepare themselues without a fault on my part: arise therefore to assist me, and beholde.
Psalm 59:5	Euen thou, O Lord God of hostes, O God of Israel awake to visit all the heathen, and be not merciful vnto all that transgresse maliciously. Selah.
Psalm 59:6	They goe to and from in the euening: they barke like dogs, and goe about the citie.
Psalm 59:7	Behold, they brag in their talke, and swords are in their lips: for, Who, say they, doeth heare?
Psalm 59:8	But thou, O Lord, shalt haue them in derision, and thou shalt laugh at all the heathen.
Psalm 59:9	He is strong: but I will waite vpon thee: for God is my defence.
Psalm 59:10	My mercifull God will preuent me: God wil let me see my desire vpon mine enemies.
Psalm 59:11	Slay them not, least my people forget it: but scatter them abroad by thy power, and put them downe, O Lord our shield,
Psalm 59:12	For the sinne of their mouth, and the words of their lips: and let them be taken in their pride, euen for their periurie and lies, that they speake.
Psalm 59:13	Consume them in thy wrath: consume them that they be no more: and let them knowe that God ruleth in Iaakob, euen vnto the ends of the world. Selah.
Psalm 59:14	And in the euening they shall go to and from, and barke like dogs, and go about the citie.
Psalm 59:15	They shall runne here and there for meate: and surely they shall not be satisfied, though they tary all night.
Psalm 59:16	But I wil sing of thy power, and will prayse thy mercy in the morning: for thou hast bene my defence and refuge in the day of my trouble.
Psalm 59:17	Vnto thee, O my Strength, wil I sing: for God is my defence, and my mercifull God.
Psalm 60:1	To him that excelleth upon Shushan Eduth, or Michtam. A Psalme of David to teach. When he fought against Aram Naharaim, and against Aram Zobah, when Joab returned and slew twelve thousand Edomites in the salt valley. O God, thou hast cast vs out, thou hast scattered vs, thou hast bene angry, turne againe vnto vs.
Psalm 60:2	Thou hast made the land to tremble, and hast made it to gape: heale the breaches thereof, for it is shaken.
Psalm 60:3	Thou hast shewed thy people heauy things: thou hast made vs to drinke the wine of giddines.
Psalm 60:4	But now thou hast giuen a banner to them that feare thee, that it may be displayed because of thy trueth. Selah.
Psalm 60:5	That thy beloued may be deliuered, helpe with thy right hand and heare me.
Psalm 60:6	God hath spoken in his holines: therefore I will reioyce: I shall deuide Shechem, and measure the valley of Succoth.
Psalm 60:7	Gilead shalbe mine, and Manasseh shalbe mine: Ephraim also shalbe the strength of mine head: Iudah is my lawgiuer.
Psalm 60:8	Moab shalbe my wash pot: ouer Edom will I cast out my shoe: Palestina shew thy selfe ioyfull for me.
Psalm 60:9	Who will leade me into the strong citie? who will bring me vnto Edom?
Psalm 60:10	Wilt not thou, O God, which hadest cast vs off, and didest not go forth, O God, with our armies?
Psalm 60:11	Giue vs helpe against trouble: for vaine is the helpe of man.
Psalm 60:12	Through God we shall doe valiantly: for he shall tread downe our enemies.
Psalm 61:1	To him that excelleth on Neginoth. A Psalme of David. Heare my cry, O God: giue eare vnto my prayer.
Psalm 61:2	From the endes of the earth will I crye vnto thee: when mine heart is opprest, bring me vpon the rocke that is higher then I.
Psalm 61:3	For thou hast bene mine hope, and a strong tower against the enemie.
Psalm 61:4	I will dwell in thy Tabernacle for euer, and my trust shall be vnder the couering of thy wings. Selah.
Psalm 61:5	For thou, O God, hast heard my desires: thou hast giuen an heritage vnto those that feare thy Name.
Psalm 61:6	Thou shalt giue the King a long life: his yeeres shalbe as many ages.
Psalm 61:7	Hee shall dwell before God for euer: prepare mercie and faithfulnes that they may preserue him.
Psalm 61:8	So will I alway sing prayse vnto thy Name in performing dayly my vowes.
Psalm 62:1	To the excelletn musician Ieduthun. A Psalme of David. Yet my soule keepeth silence vnto God: of him commeth my saluation.
Psalm 62:2	Yet he is my strength and my saluation, and my defence: therefore I shall not much be mooued.
Psalm 62:3	How long wil ye imagine mischiefe against a man? ye shalbe all slaine: ye shalbe as a bowed wall, or as a wall shaken.
Psalm 62:4	Yet they consult to cast him downe from his dignitie: their delight is in lies, they blesse with their mouthes, but curse with their hearts. Selah.
Psalm 62:5	Yet my soule keepe thou silence vnto God: for mine hope is in him.
Psalm 62:6	Yet is hee my strength, and my saluation, and my defence: therefore I shall not be mooued.
Psalm 62:7	In God is my saluation and my glory, the rocke of my strength: in God is my trust.
Psalm 62:8	Trust in him alway, ye people: powre out your hearts before him, for God is our hope. Selah.
Psalm 62:9	Yet the children of men are vanitie, the chiefe men are lies: to lay them vpon a balance they are altogether lighter then vanitie.
Psalm 62:10	Trust not in oppression nor in robberie: be not vaine: if riches increase, set not your heart thereon.
Psalm 62:11	God spake once or twise, I haue heard it, that power belongeth vnto God,
Psalm 62:12	And to thee, O Lord, mercie: for thou rewardest euery one according to his worke.
Psalm 63:1	A Psalme of David. When he was in the wildernesse of Judah. O God, thou art my God, earely will I seeke thee: my soule thirsteth for thee: my flesh longeth greatly after thee in a barren and drye land without water.
Psalm 63:2	Thus I beholde thee as in the Sanctuarie, when I beholde thy power and thy glorie.
Psalm 63:3	For thy louing kindnesse is better then life: therefore my lippes shall prayse thee.
Psalm 63:4	Thus will I magnifie thee all my life, and lift vp mine hands in thy name.
Psalm 63:5	My soule shalbe satisfied, as with marowe and fatnesse, and my mouth shall praise thee with ioyfull lippes,
Psalm 63:6	When I remember thee on my bedde, and when I thinke vpon thee in the night watches.
Psalm 63:7	Because thou hast bene mine helper, therefore vnder the shadow of thy wings wil I reioyce.
Psalm 63:8	My soule cleaueth vnto thee: for thy right hand vpholdeth me.
Psalm 63:9	Therefore they that seeke my soule to destroy it, they shall goe into the lowest partes of the earth.
Psalm 63:10	They shall cast him downe with the edge of the sword, and they shall be a portion for foxes.
Psalm 63:11	But the King shall reioyce in God, and all that sweare by him shall reioyce in him: for the mouth of them that speake lyes, shall be stopped.
Psalm 64:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of David. Heare my voyce, O God, in my prayer: preserue my life from feare of the enemie.
Psalm 64:2	Hide me from the conspiracie of the wicked, and from the rage of the workers of iniquitie.
Psalm 64:3	Which haue whette their tongue like a sword, and shot for their arrowes bitter wordes.
Psalm 64:4	To shoote at the vpright in secrete: they shoote at him suddenly, and feare not.
Psalm 64:5	They encourage themselues in a wicked purpose: they commune together to lay snares priuilie, and say, Who shall see them?
Psalm 64:6	They haue sought out iniquities, and haue accomplished that which they sought out, euen euery one his secret thoughtes, and the depth of his heart.
Psalm 64:7	But God will shoote an arrowe at them suddenly: their strokes shalbe at once.
Psalm 64:8	They shall cause their owne tongue to fall vpon them: and whosoeuer shall see them, shall flee away.
Psalm 64:9	And all men shall see it, and declare the worke of God, and they shall vnderstand, what he hath wrought.
Psalm 64:10	But the righteous shall be glad in the Lord, and trust in him: and all that are vpright of heart, shall reioyce.
Psalm 65:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme or song of David. O God, praise waiteth for thee in Zion, and vnto thee shall the vowe be perfourmed.
Psalm 65:2	Because thou hearest the prayer, vnto thee shall all flesh come.
Psalm 65:3	Wicked deedes haue preuailed against me: but thou wilt be mercifull vnto our transgressions.
Psalm 65:4	Blessed is he, whom thou chusest and causest to come to thee: he shall dwell in thy courtes, and we shall be satisfied with the pleasures of thine House, euen of thine holy Temple.
Psalm 65:5	O God of our saluation, thou wilt answere vs with fearefull signes in thy righteousnes, O thou the hope of all the ends of the earth, and of them that are farre off in the sea.
Psalm 65:6	He stablisheth the mountaines by his power: and is girded about with strength.
Psalm 65:7	He appeaseth the noyse of the seas and the noyse of the waues thereof, and the tumults of the people.
Psalm 65:8	They also, that dwell in the vttermost parts of the earth, shalbe afraide of thy signes: thou shalt make the East and the West to reioyce.
Psalm 65:9	Thou visitest the earth, and waterest it: thou makest it very riche: the Riuer of God is full of water: thou preparest them corne: for so thou appointest it.
Psalm 65:10	Thou waterest abundantly the furrowes thereof: thou causest the raine to descende into the valleies thereof: thou makest it soft with showres, and blessest the bud thereof.
Psalm 65:11	Thou crownest ye yeere with thy goodnesse, and thy steppes droppe fatnesse.
Psalm 65:12	They drop vpon the pastures of the wildernesse: and the hils shalbe compassed with gladnes.
Psalm 65:13	The pastures are clad with sheepe: the valleis also shalbe couered with corne: therefore they shoute for ioye, and sing.
Psalm 66:1	To him that excelleth. A song or Psalme. Rejoice in God, all ye inhabitants of the earth.
Psalm 66:2	Sing forth the glory of his name: make his praise glorious.
Psalm 66:3	Say vnto God, Howe terrible art thou in thy workes! through the greatnesse of thy power shall thine enemies be in subiection vnto thee.
Psalm 66:4	All the worlde shall worship thee, and sing vnto thee, euen sing of thy Name. Selah.
Psalm 66:5	Come and beholde the workes of God: he is terrible in his doing towarde the sonnes of men.
Psalm 66:6	He hath turned the Sea into drie land: they passe through the riuer on foote: there did we reioyce in him.
Psalm 66:7	He ruleth the worlde with his power: his eyes beholde the nations: the rebellious shall not exalt them selues. Selah.
Psalm 66:8	Prayse our God, ye people, and make the voyce of his prayse to be heard.
Psalm 66:9	Which holdeth our soules in life, and suffereth not our feete to slippe.
Psalm 66:10	For thou, O God, hast proued vs, thou hast tryed vs as siluer is tryed.
Psalm 66:11	Thou hast brought vs into the snare, and layed a strait chaine vpon our loynes.
Psalm 66:12	Thou hast caused men to ryde ouer our heads: we went into fire and into water, but thou broughtest vs out into a welthie place.
Psalm 66:13	I will go into thine House with burnt offrings, and will pay thee my vowes,
Psalm 66:14	Which my lippes haue promised, and my mouth hath spoken in mine affliction.
Psalm 66:15	I will offer vnto thee the burnt offerings of fat rammes with incense: I will prepare bullocks and goates. Selah.
Psalm 66:16	Come and hearken, all ye that feare God, and I will tell you what he hath done to my soule.
Psalm 66:17	I called vnto him with my mouth, and he was exalted with my tongue.
Psalm 66:18	If I regard wickednesse in mine heart, the Lord will not heare me.
Psalm 66:19	But God hath heard me, and considered the voyce of my prayer.
Psalm 66:20	Praysed be God, which hath not put backe my prayer, nor his mercie from me.
Psalm 67:1	To him that excelleth on Neginoth. A Psalme or song. God be mercifull vnto vs, and blesse vs, and cause his face to shine among vs. Selah.
Psalm 67:2	That they may know thy way vpon earth, and thy sauing health among all nations.
Psalm 67:3	Let the people prayse thee, O God: let all the people prayse thee.
Psalm 67:4	Let the people be glad and reioyce: for thou shalt iudge the people righteously, and gouerne the nations vpon the earth. Selah.
Psalm 67:5	Let the people prayse thee, O God: let all the people prayse thee.
Psalm 67:6	Then shall the earth bring foorth her increase, and God, euen our God shall blesse vs.
Psalm 67:7	God shall blesse vs, and all the endes of the earth shall feare him.
Psalm 68:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme or song of David. God will arise, and his enemies shalbe scattered: they also that hate him, shall flee before him.
Psalm 68:2	As the smoke vanisheth, so shalt thou driue them away: and as waxe melteth before the fire, so shall the wicked perish at the presence of God.
Psalm 68:3	But the righteous shalbe glad, and reioyce before God: yea, they shall leape for ioye.
Psalm 68:4	Sing vnto God, and sing prayses vnto his name: exalt him that rideth vpon the heauens, in his Name Iah, and reioyce before him.
Psalm 68:5	He is a Father of the fatherlesse, and a Iudge of the widowes, euen God in his holy habitation.
Psalm 68:6	God maketh the solitarie to dwell in families, and deliuereth them that were prisoners in stocks: but the rebellious shall dwell in a dry land.
Psalm 68:7	O God, when thou wentest forth before thy people: when thou wentest through the wildernesse, (Selah)
Psalm 68:8	The earth shooke, and the heauens dropped at the presence of this God: euen Sinai was moued at the presence of God, euen the God of Israel.
Psalm 68:9	Thou, O God, sendest a gracious raine vpon thine inheritance, and thou didest refresh it when it was wearie.
Psalm 68:10	Thy Congregation dwelled therein: for thou, O God, hast of thy goodnesse prepared it for the poore.
Psalm 68:11	The Lord gaue matter to the women to tell of the great armie.
Psalm 68:12	Kings of the armies did flee: they did flee, and she that remained in the house, deuided the spoyle.
Psalm 68:13	Though ye haue lien among pots, yet shall ye be as the winges of a doue that is couered with siluer, and whose fethers are like yelowe golde.
Psalm 68:14	When the Almightie scattered Kings in it, it was white as the snowe in Zalmon.
Psalm 68:15	The mountaine of God is like the mountaine of Bashan: it is an high Mountaine, as mount Bashan.
Psalm 68:16	Why leape ye, ye high mountaines? as for this Mountaine, God deliteth to dwell in it: yea, the Lord will dwell in it for euer.
Psalm 68:17	The charets of God are twentie thousande thousand Angels, and the Lord is among them, as in the Sanctuarie of Sinai.
Psalm 68:18	Thou art gone vp on high: thou hast led captiuitie captiue, and receiued giftes for men: yea, euen the rebellious hast thou led, that the Lord God might dwell there.
Psalm 68:19	Praysed be the Lord, euen the God of our saluation, which ladeth vs dayly with benefites. Selah.
Psalm 68:20	This is our God, euen the God that saueth vs: and to the Lord God belong the issues of death.
Psalm 68:21	Surely God will wound the head of his enemies, and the hearie pate of him that walketh in his sinnes.
Psalm 68:22	The Lord hath sayde, I will bring my people againe from Bashan: I will bring them againe from the depths of the Sea:
Psalm 68:23	That thy foote may bee dipped in blood, and the tongue of thy dogges in the blood of the enemies, euen in it.
Psalm 68:24	They haue seene, O God, thy goings, the goings of my God, and my King, which art in the Sanctuarie.
Psalm 68:25	The singers went before, the players of instruments after: in the middes were the maides playing with timbrels.
Psalm 68:26	Praise yee God in the assemblies, and the Lord, ye that are of the fountaine of Israel.
Psalm 68:27	There was litle Beniamin with their ruler, and the princes of Iudah with their assemblie, the princes of Zebulun, and the princes of Naphtali.
Psalm 68:28	Thy God hath appointed thy strength: stablish, O God, that, which thou hast wrought in vs,
Psalm 68:29	Out of thy Temple vpon Ierusalem: and Kings shall bring presents vnto thee.
Psalm 68:30	Destroy the company of the spearemen, and multitude of the mightie bulles with the calues of the people, that tread vnder feete pieces of siluer: scatter the people that delite in warre.
Psalm 68:31	Then shall the princes come out of Egypt: Ethiopia shall hast to stretche her hands vnto God.
Psalm 68:32	Sing vnto God, O yee kingdomes of the earth: sing praise vnto the Lord, (Selah)
Psalm 68:33	To him that rideth vpon ye most high heauens, which were from the beginning: beholde, he will send out by his voice a mightie sound.
Psalm 68:34	Ascribe the power to God: for his maiestie is vpon Israel, and his strength is in the cloudes.
Psalm 68:35	O God, thou art terrible out of thine holie places: the God of Israel is hee that giueth strength and power vnto the people: praised be God.
Psalm 69:1	To him that excelleth upon Shoshannim. A Psalme of David. Save mee, O God: for the waters are entred euen to my soule.
Psalm 69:2	I sticke fast in the deepe myre, where no staie is: I am come into deepe waters, and the streames runne ouer me.
Psalm 69:3	I am wearie of crying: my throte is drie: mine eyes faile, whiles I waite for my God.
Psalm 69:4	They that hate mee without a cause, are moe then the heares of mine heade: they that would destroy mee, and are mine enemies falsly, are mightie, so that I restored that which I tooke not.
Psalm 69:5	O God, thou knowest my foolishnesse, and my fautes are not hid from thee.
Psalm 69:6	Let not them that trust in thee, O Lord God of hostes, be ashamed for me: let not those that seeke thee, be confounded through mee, O God of Israel.
Psalm 69:7	For thy sake haue I suffred reproofe: shame hath couered my face.
Psalm 69:8	I am become a stranger vnto my brethren, euen an aliant vnto my mothers sonnes.
Psalm 69:9	For the zeale of thine house hath eaten mee, and the rebukes of them that rebuked thee, are fallen vpon me.
Psalm 69:10	I wept and my soule fasted, but that was to my reproofe.
Psalm 69:11	I put on a sacke also: and I became a prouerbe vnto them.
Psalm 69:12	They that sate in the gate, spake of mee, and the drunkards sang of me.
Psalm 69:13	But Lord, I make my praier vnto thee in an acceptable time, euen in the multitude of thy mercie: O God, heare me in the trueth of thy saluation.
Psalm 69:14	Deliuer mee out of the myre, that I sinke not: let me be deliuered from them that hate me, and out of the deepe waters.
Psalm 69:15	Let not the water flood drowne mee, neither let the deepe swallowe me vp: and let not the pit shut her mouth vpon me.
Psalm 69:16	Heare me, O Lord, for thy louing kindnes is good: turne vnto me according to ye multitude of thy tender mercies.
Psalm 69:17	And hide not thy face from thy seruant, for I am in trouble: make haste and heare me.
Psalm 69:18	Draw neere vnto my soule and redeeme it: deliuer me because of mine enemies.
Psalm 69:19	Thou hast knowen my reproofe and my shame, and my dishonour: all mine aduersaries are before thee.
Psalm 69:20	Rebuke hath broken mine heart, and I am full of heauinesse, and I looked for some to haue pitie on me, but there was none: and for comforters, but I found none.
Psalm 69:21	For they gaue me gall in my meate, and in my thirst they gaue me vineger to drinke.
Psalm 69:22	Let their table be a snare before them, and their prosperitie their ruine.
Psalm 69:23	Let their eyes be blinded that they see not: and make their loynes alway to tremble.
Psalm 69:24	Powre out thine anger vpon them, and let thy wrathfull displeasure take them.
Psalm 69:25	Let their habitation be voide, and let none dwell in their tents.
Psalm 69:26	For they persecute him, whome thou hast smitten: and they adde vnto the sorrowe of them, whome thou hast wounded.
Psalm 69:27	Laie iniquitie vpon their iniquitie, and let them not come into thy righteousnesse.
Psalm 69:28	Let them be put out of the booke of life, neither let them be written with the righteous.
Psalm 69:29	When I am poore and in heauinesse, thine helpe, O God, shall exalt me.
Psalm 69:30	I will praise the Name of God with a song, and magnifie him with thankesgiuing.
Psalm 69:31	This also shall please the Lord better then a yong bullocke, that hath hornes and hoofes.
Psalm 69:32	The humble shall see this, and they that seeke God, shalbe glad, and your heart shall liue.
Psalm 69:33	For the Lord heareth the poore, and despiseth not his prisoners.
Psalm 69:34	Let heauen and earth praise him: the seas and all that moueth in them.
Psalm 69:35	For God will saue Zion, and builde the cities of Iudah, that men may dwell there and haue it in possession.
Psalm 69:36	The seede also of his seruants shall inherit it: and they that loue his name, shall dwel therein.
Psalm 70:1	To him excelleth. A Psalme of David to put in remembrance. O God, haste thee to deliuer mee: make haste to helpe me, O Lord.
Psalm 70:2	Let them be confounded and put to shame, that seeke my soule: let them bee turned backewarde and put to rebuke, that desire mine hurt.
Psalm 70:3	Let them be turned backe for a rewarde of their shame, which said, Aha, aha.
Psalm 70:4	But let all those that seeke thee, be ioyfull and glad in thee, and let all that loue thy saluation, say alwaies, God be praised.
Psalm 70:5	Nowe I am poore and needie: O God, make haste to me: thou art mine helper, and my deliuerer: O Lord, make no tarying.
Psalm 71:1	In thee, O Lord, I trust: let me neuer be ashamed.
Psalm 71:2	Rescue mee and deliuer me in thy righteousnes: incline thine eare vnto me and saue me.
Psalm 71:3	Be thou my strong rocke, whereunto I may alway resort: thou hast giuen commandement to saue me: for thou art my rocke, and my fortresse.
Psalm 71:4	Deliuer mee, O my God, out of the hande of the wicked: out of the hande of the euill and cruell man.
Psalm 71:5	For thou art mine hope, O Lord God, euen my trust from my youth.
Psalm 71:6	Vpon thee haue I beene stayed from the wombe: thou art he that tooke me out of my mothers bowels: my praise shalbe alwaies of thee.
Psalm 71:7	I am become as it were a monster vnto many: but thou art my sure trust.
Psalm 71:8	Let my mouth be filled with thy praise, and with thy glory euery day.
Psalm 71:9	Cast mee not off in the time of age: forsake me not when my strength faileth.
Psalm 71:10	For mine enemies speake of mee, and they that lay waite for my soule, take their counsell together,
Psalm 71:11	Saying, God hath forsaken him: pursue and take him, for there is none to deliuer him.
Psalm 71:12	Goe not farre from me, O God: my God, haste thee to helpe me.
Psalm 71:13	Let them be confounded and consumed that are against my soule: let them be couered with reproofe and confusion, that seeke mine hurt.
Psalm 71:14	But I will waite continually, and will praise thee more and more.
Psalm 71:15	My mouth shall daily rehearse thy righteousnesse, and thy saluation: for I knowe not the nomber.
Psalm 71:16	I will goe forwarde in the strength of the Lord God, and will make mention of thy righteousnesse, euen of thine onely.
Psalm 71:17	O God, thou hast taught me from my youth euen vntill nowe: therefore will I tell of thy wonderous workes,
Psalm 71:18	Yea, euen vnto mine olde age and graie head, O God: forsake me not, vntill I haue declared thine arme vnto this generation, and thy power to all them, that shall come.
Psalm 71:19	And thy righteousnes, O God, I wil exalt on high: for thou hast done great thinges: O God, who is like vnto thee!
Psalm 71:20	Which hast shewed me great troubles and aduersities, but thou wilt returne, and reuiue me, and wilt come againe, and take mee vp from the depth of the earth.
Psalm 71:21	Thou wilt increase mine honour, and returne and comfort me.
Psalm 71:22	Therefore will I praise thee for thy faithfulnesse, O God, vpon instrument and viole: vnto thee will I sing vpon the harpe, O Holy one of Israel.
Psalm 71:23	My lippes will reioyce when I sing vnto thee, and my soule, which thou hast deliuered.
Psalm 71:24	My tongue also shall talke of thy righteousnesse daily: for they are confounded and brought vnto shame, that seeke mine hurt.
Psalm 72:1	A Psalme of Salomon. Give thy iudgements to the King, O God, and thy righteousnesse to the Kings sonne.
Psalm 72:2	Then shall he iudge thy people in righteousnesse, and thy poore with equitie.
Psalm 72:3	The mountaines and the hilles shall bring peace to the people by iustice.
Psalm 72:4	He shall iudge the poore of the people: he shall saue the children of the needie, and shall subdue the oppressor.
Psalm 72:5	They shall feare thee as long as the sunne and moone endureth, from generatio to generation.
Psalm 72:6	He shall come downe like the rayne vpon the mowen grasse, and as the showres that water the earth.
Psalm 72:7	In his dayes shall the righteous florish, and abundance of peace shalbe so long as the moone endureth.
Psalm 72:8	His dominion shall be also from sea to sea, and from the Riuer vnto the endes of the land.
Psalm 72:9	They that dwell in ye wildernes, shall kneele before him, and his enemies shall licke the dust.
Psalm 72:10	The Kings of Tarshish and of the yles shall bring presents: the Kings of Sheba and Seba shall bring giftes.
Psalm 72:11	Yea, all Kings shall worship him: all nations shall serue him.
Psalm 72:12	For he shall deliuer the poore when he cryeth: the needie also, and him that hath no helper.
Psalm 72:13	He shalbe mercifull to the poore and needie, and shall preserue the soules of the poore.
Psalm 72:14	He shall redeeme their soules from deceite and violence, and deare shall their blood be in his sight.
Psalm 72:15	Yea, he shall liue, and vnto him shall they giue of the golde of Sheba: they shall also pray for him continually, and dayly blesse him.
Psalm 72:16	An handfull of corne shall be sowen in the earth, euen in the toppe of the mountaines, and the fruite thereof shall shake like the trees of Lebanon: and the children shall florish out of the citie like the grasse of the earth.
Psalm 72:17	His name shall be for euer: his name shall indure as long as the sunne: all nations shall blesse him, and be blessed in him.
Psalm 72:18	Blessed be the Lord God, euen the God of Israel, which onely doeth wonderous things.
Psalm 72:19	And blessed be his glorious Name for euer: and let all the earth be filled with his glorie. So be it, euen so be it. HERE END THE prayers of Dauid, the sonne of Ishai.
Psalm 73:1	A Psalme committed to Asaph. Yet God is good to Israel: euen, to the pure in heart.
Psalm 73:2	As for me, my feete were almost gone: my steps had well neere slipt.
Psalm 73:3	For I feared at the foolish, when I sawe the prosperitie of the wicked.
Psalm 73:4	For there are no bandes in their death, but they are lustie and strong.
Psalm 73:5	They are not in trouble as other men, neither are they plagued with other men.
Psalm 73:6	Therefore pride is as a chayne vnto them, and crueltie couereth them as a garment.
Psalm 73:7	Their eyes stande out for fatnesse: they haue more then heart can wish.
Psalm 73:8	They are licentious, and speake wickedly of their oppression: they talke presumptuously.
Psalm 73:9	They set their mouth against heauen, and their tongue walketh through the earth.
Psalm 73:10	Therefore his people turne hither: for waters of a full cup are wrung out to them.
Psalm 73:11	And they say, Howe doeth God know it? or is there knowledge in the most High?
Psalm 73:12	Lo, these are the wicked, yet prosper they alway, and increase in riches.
Psalm 73:13	Certainely I haue clensed mine heart in vaine, and washed mine hands in innocencie.
Psalm 73:14	For dayly haue I bene punished, and chastened euery morning.
Psalm 73:15	If I say, I will iudge thus, beholde the generation of thy children: I haue trespassed.
Psalm 73:16	Then thought I to know this, but it was too painefull for me,
Psalm 73:17	Vntill I went into the Sanctuarie of God: then vnderstoode I their ende.
Psalm 73:18	Surely thou hast set them in slipperie places, and castest them downe into desolation.
Psalm 73:19	How suddenly are they destroyed, perished and horribly consumed,
Psalm 73:20	As a dreame when one awaketh! O Lord, when thou raisest vs vp, thou shalt make their image despised.
Psalm 73:21	Certainely mine heart was vexed, and I was pricked in my reines:
Psalm 73:22	So foolish was I and ignorant: I was a beast before thee.
Psalm 73:23	Yet I was alway with thee: thou hast holden me by my right hand.
Psalm 73:24	Thou wilt guide me by thy counsell, and afterward receiue me to glory.
Psalm 73:25	Whom haue I in heauen but thee? and I haue desired none in the earth with thee.
Psalm 73:26	My flesh fayleth and mine heart also: but God is the strength of mine heart, and my portion for euer.
Psalm 73:27	For loe, they that withdrawe themselues from thee, shall perish: thou destroyest all them that goe a whoring from thee.
Psalm 73:28	As for me, it is good for me to draw neere to God: therefore I haue put my trust in the Lord God, that I may declare all thy workes.
Psalm 74:1	A Psalme to give instruction, committed to Asaph. O God, why hast thou put vs away for euer? why is thy wrath kindled against the sheepe of thy pasture?
Psalm 74:2	Thinke vpon thy Congregation, which thou hast possessed of olde, and on the rod of thine inheritance, which thou hast redeemed, and on this mount Zion, wherein thou hast dwelt.
Psalm 74:3	Lift vp thy strokes, that thou mayest for euer destroy euery enemie that doeth euill to the Sanctuarie.
Psalm 74:4	Thine aduersaries roare in the middes of thy Congregation, and set vp their banners for signes.
Psalm 74:5	He that lifted the axes vpon the thicke trees, was renowmed, as one, that brought a thing to perfection:
Psalm 74:6	But nowe they breake downe the carued worke thereof with axes and hammers.
Psalm 74:7	They haue cast thy Sanctuarie into the fire, and rased it to the grounde, and haue defiled the dwelling place of thy Name.
Psalm 74:8	They saide in their hearts, Let vs destroy them altogether: they haue burnt all the Synagogues of God in the land.
Psalm 74:9	We see not our signes: there is not one Prophet more, nor any with vs that knoweth howe long.
Psalm 74:10	O God, howe long shall the aduersarie reproche thee? shall the enemie blaspheme thy Name for euer?
Psalm 74:11	Why withdrawest thou thine hand, euen thy right hand? drawe it out of thy bosome, and consume them.
Psalm 74:12	Euen God is my King of olde, working saluation in the middes of the earth.
Psalm 74:13	Thou didest deuide the sea by thy power: thou brakest the heads of the dragons in the waters.
Psalm 74:14	Thou brakest the head of Liuiathan in pieces, and gauest him to be meate for the people in wildernesse.
Psalm 74:15	Thou brakest vp the fountaine and riuer: thou dryedst vp mightie riuers.
Psalm 74:16	The day is thine, and the night is thine: thou hast prepared the light and the sunne.
Psalm 74:17	Thou hast set all the borders of the earth: thou hast made summer and winter.
Psalm 74:18	Remember this, that the enemie hath reproched the Lord, and the foolish people hath blasphemed thy Name.
Psalm 74:19	Giue not the soule of thy turtle doue vnto the beast, and forget not the Congregation of thy poore for euer.
Psalm 74:20	Consider thy couenant: for the darke places of the earth are full of the habitations of the cruell.
Psalm 74:21	Oh let not the oppressed returne ashamed, but let the poore and needie prayse thy Name.
Psalm 74:22	Arise, O God: mainteine thine owne cause: remember thy dayly reproche by the foolish man.
Psalm 74:23	Forget not the voyce of thine enemies: for the tumult of them, that rise against thee, ascendeth continually.
Psalm 75:1	To him that excelleth. Destroy not. A Psalme or song committed toAsaph. We will prayse thee, O God, we will prayse thee, for thy Name is neere: therefore they will declare thy wonderous workes.
Psalm 75:2	When I shall take a conuenient time, I will iudge righteously.
Psalm 75:3	The earth and all the inhabitantes thereof are dissolued: but I will establish the pillars of it. Selah.
Psalm 75:4	I saide vnto the foolish, Be not so foolish, and to the wicked, Lift not vp the horne.
Psalm 75:5	Lift not vp your horne on high, neither speake with a stiffe necke.
Psalm 75:6	For to come to preferment is neither from the East, nor from the West, nor from the South,
Psalm 75:7	But God is the iudge: he maketh lowe and he maketh hie.
Psalm 75:8	For in the hand of the Lord is a cup, and the wine is red: it is full mixt, and he powreth out of the same: surely all the wicked of the earth shall wring out and drinke the dregges thereof.
Psalm 75:9	But I will declare for euer, and sing prayses vnto the God of Iaakob.
Psalm 75:10	All the hornes of the wicked also will I breake: but the hornes of the righteous shalbe exalted.
Psalm 76:1	To him that excelleth on Neginoth. A Psalme or song committed to Asaph. God is knowen in Iudah: his Name is great in Israel.
Psalm 76:2	For in Shalem is his Tabernacle, and his dwelling in Zion.
Psalm 76:3	There brake he the arrowes of the bowe, the shielde and the sword and the battell. Selah.
Psalm 76:4	Thou art more bright and puissant, then the mountaines of pray.
Psalm 76:5	The stout hearted are spoyled: they haue slept their sleepe, and all the men of strength haue not found their hands.
Psalm 76:6	At thy rebuke, O God of Iaakob, both the chariot and horse are cast a sleepe.
Psalm 76:7	Thou, euen thou art to be feared: and who shall stand in thy sight, when thou art angrie!
Psalm 76:8	Thou didest cause thy iudgement to bee heard from heauen: therefore the earth feared and was still,
Psalm 76:9	When thou, O God, arose to iudgement, to helpe all the meeke of the earth. Selah.
Psalm 76:10	Surely the rage of man shall turne to thy praise: the remnant of the rage shalt thou restrayne.
Psalm 76:11	Vowe and performe vnto the Lord your God, all ye that be rounde about him: let them bring presents vnto him that ought to be feared.
Psalm 76:12	He shall cut off the spirit of princes: he is terrible to the Kings of the earth.
Psalm 77:1	For the excellent musician Ieduthun. A Psalme committed to Asaph. My voyce came to God, when I cryed: my voyce came to God, and he heard me.
Psalm 77:2	In the day of my trouble I sought ye Lord: my sore ranne and ceased not in the night: my soule refused comfort.
Psalm 77:3	I did thinke vpon God, and was troubled: I praied, and my spirit was full of anguish. Selah.
Psalm 77:4	Thou keepest mine eyes waking: I was astonied and could not speake.
Psalm 77:5	Then I considered the daies of olde, and the yeeres of ancient time.
Psalm 77:6	I called to remembrance my song in the night: I communed with mine owne heart, and my spirit searched diligently.
Psalm 77:7	Will the Lord absent him selfe for euer? and will he shewe no more fauour?
Psalm 77:8	Is his mercie cleane gone for euer? doeth his promise faile for euermore?
Psalm 77:9	Hath God forgotten to be mercifull? hath he shut vp his teder mercies in displeasure? Selah.
Psalm 77:10	And I sayde, This is my death: yet I remembred the yeeres of the right hand of the most High.
Psalm 77:11	I remembred the workes of the Lord: certainely I remembred thy wonders of olde.
Psalm 77:12	I did also meditate all thy woorkes, and did deuise of thine actes, saying,
Psalm 77:13	Thy way, O God, is in the Sanctuarie: who is so great a God as our God!
Psalm 77:14	Thou art ye God that doest wonders: thou hast declared thy power among the people.
Psalm 77:15	Thou hast redeemed thy people with thine arme, euen the sonnes of Iaakob and Ioseph. Selah.
Psalm 77:16	The waters sawe thee, O God: the waters sawe thee, and were afraide: yea, the depths trembled.
Psalm 77:17	The cloudes powred out water: the heauens gaue a sounde: yea, thine arrowes went abroade.
Psalm 77:18	The voyce of thy thunder was rounde about: the lightnings lightened the worlde: the earth trembled and shooke.
Psalm 77:19	Thy way is in the Sea, and thy paths in the great waters, and thy footesteps are not knowen.
Psalm 77:20	Thou diddest leade thy people like sheepe by the hand of Moses and Aaron.
