Psalm 78:1	A Psalme to give instruction committed to Asaph. Heare my doctrine, O my people: incline your eares vnto the wordes of my mouth.
Psalm 78:2	I will open my mouth in a parable: I will declare high sentences of olde.
Psalm 78:3	Which we haue heard and knowen, and our fathers haue tolde vs.
Psalm 78:4	Wee will not hide them from their children but to the generation to come we wil shewe the praises of the Lord his power also, and his wonderful woorkes that he hath done:
Psalm 78:5	How he established a testimonie in Iaakob, and ordeined a Law in Israel, which he commanded our fathers, that they shoulde teache their children:
Psalm 78:6	That the posteritie might knowe it, and the children, which should be borne, should stand vp, and declare it to their children:
Psalm 78:7	That they might set their hope on God, and not forget the workes of God but keepe his commandements:
Psalm 78:8	And not to bee as their fathers, a disobedient and rebellious generation: a generation that set not their heart aright, and whose spirite was not faithfull vnto God.
Psalm 78:9	The children of Ephraim being armed and shooting with the bowe, turned backe in the day of battell.
Psalm 78:10	They kept not the couenant of God, but refused to walke in his Lawe,
Psalm 78:11	And forgate his Actes, and his wonderfull woorkes that he had shewed them.
Psalm 78:12	Hee did marueilous thinges in the sight of their fathers in the lande of Egypt: euen in the fielde of Zoan.
Psalm 78:13	He deuided the Sea, and led them through: he made also the waters to stand as an heape.
Psalm 78:14	In the day time also hee led them with a cloude, and all the night with a light of fire.
Psalm 78:15	He claue the rockes in the wildernes, and gaue them drinke as of the great depths.
Psalm 78:16	He brought floods also out of the stonie rocke; so that hee made the waters to descend like the riuers.
Psalm 78:17	Yet they sinned stil against him, and prouoked the Highest in the wildernesse,
Psalm 78:18	And tempted God in their heartes in requiring meate for their lust.
Psalm 78:19	They spake against God also, saying, Can God prepare a table in the wildernesse?
Psalm 78:20	Behold, he smote the rocke, that the water gushed out, and the streames ouerflowed: can hee giue bread also? or prepare flesh for his people?
Psalm 78:21	Therefore the Lord heard and was angrie, and the fire was kindled in Iaakob, and also wrath came vpon Israel,
Psalm 78:22	Because they beleeued not in God, and trusted not in his helpe.
Psalm 78:23	Yet he had comanded the clouds aboue, and had opened the doores of heauen,
Psalm 78:24	And had rained downe MAN vpon them for to eate, and had giuen them of the wheate of heauen.
Psalm 78:25	Man did eate the bread of Angels: hee sent them meate ynough.
Psalm 78:26	He caused the Eastwinde to passe in the heauen, and through his power he brought in the Southwinde.
Psalm 78:27	Hee rained flesh also vpon them as dust, and feathered foule as the sand of the sea.
Psalm 78:28	And hee made it fall in the middes of their campe euen round about their habitations.
Psalm 78:29	So they did eate and were well filled: for he gaue them their desire.
Psalm 78:30	They were not turned from their lust, but the meate was yet in their mouthes,
Psalm 78:31	When the wrath of God came euen vpon them, and slew the strongest of them, and smote downe the chosen men in Israel.
Psalm 78:32	For all this, they sinned stil, and beleeued not his wonderous woorkes.
Psalm 78:33	Therefore their daies did hee consume in vanitie, and their yeeres hastily.
Psalm 78:34	And when hee slewe them, they sought him and they returned, and sought God earely.
Psalm 78:35	And they remembred that God was their strength, and the most high God their redeemer.
Psalm 78:36	But they flattered him with their mouth, and dissembled with him with their tongue.
Psalm 78:37	For their heart was not vpright with him: neither were they faithfull in his couenant.
Psalm 78:38	Yet he being merciful forgaue their iniquitie, and destroied them not, but oft times called backe his anger, and did not stirre vp all his wrath.
Psalm 78:39	For he remembered that they were flesh: yea, a winde that passeth and commeth not againe.
Psalm 78:40	How oft did they prouoke him in the wildernes? and grieue him in the desert?
Psalm 78:41	Yea, they returned, and tempted God, and limited the Holie one of Israel.
Psalm 78:42	They remembered not his hand, nor the day when he deliuered them from the enemie,
Psalm 78:43	Nor him that set his signes in Egypt, and his wonders in the fielde of Zoan,
Psalm 78:44	And turned their riuers into blood, and their floods, that they could not drinke.
Psalm 78:45	Hee sent a swarme of flies among them, which deuoured them, and frogs, which destroyed them.
Psalm 78:46	He gaue also their fruites vnto the caterpiller, and their labour vnto the grassehopper.
Psalm 78:47	He destroied their vines with haile, and their wilde figge trees with the hailestone.
Psalm 78:48	He gaue their cattell also to the haile, and their flockes to the thunderboltes.
Psalm 78:49	Hee cast vpon them the fiercenesse of his anger, indignation and wrath, and vexation by the sending out of euill Angels.
Psalm 78:50	He made a way to his anger: he spared not their soule from death, but gaue their life to the pestilence,
Psalm 78:51	And smote al the firstborne in Egypt, euen the beginning of their strength in the tabernacles of Ham.
Psalm 78:52	But hee made his people to goe out like sheepe, and led them in the wildernes like a flocke.
Psalm 78:53	Yea, he caried them out safely, and they feared not, and the Sea couered their enemies.
Psalm 78:54	And he brought them vnto the borders of his Sanctuarie: euen to this Mountaine, which his right hand purchased.
Psalm 78:55	He cast out the heathe also before them, and caused them to fall to the lot of his inheritance, and made the tribes of Israel to dwell in their tabernacles.
Psalm 78:56	Yet they tempted, and prouoked the most high God, and kept not his testimonies,
Psalm 78:57	But turned backe and delt falsely like their fathers: they turned like a deceitfull bowe.
Psalm 78:58	And they prouoked him to anger with their high places, and mooued him to wrath with their grauen images.
Psalm 78:59	God heard this and was wroth, and greatly abhorred Israel,
Psalm 78:60	So that hee forsooke the habitation of Shilo, euen the Tabernacle where hee dwelt among men,
Psalm 78:61	And deliuered his power into captiuitie, and his beautie into the enemies hand.
Psalm 78:62	And hee gaue vp his people to the sworde, and was angrie with his inheritance.
Psalm 78:63	The fire deuoured their chosen men, and their maides were not praised.
Psalm 78:64	Their Priestes fell by the sworde, and their widowes lamented not.
Psalm 78:65	But the Lord awaked as one out of sleepe, and as a strong man that after his wine crieth out,
Psalm 78:66	And smote his enemies in the hinder parts, and put them to a perpetuall shame.
Psalm 78:67	Yet he refused the tabernacle of Ioseph, and chose not the tribe of Ephraim:
Psalm 78:68	But chose the tribe of Iudah, and mount Zion which he loued.
Psalm 78:69	And he built his Sanctuarie as an high palace, like the earth, which he stablished for euer.
Psalm 78:70	He chose Dauid also his seruant, and tooke him from the shepefolds.
Psalm 78:71	Euen from behinde the ewes with yong brought he him to feede his people in Iaakob, and his inheritance in Israel.
Psalm 78:72	So he fed them according to the simplicitie of his heart, and guided them by the discretion of his hands.
Psalm 79:1	A Psalme committed to Asaph. O God, the heathen are come into thine inheritance: thine holy Temple haue they defiled, and made Ierusalem heapes of stones.
Psalm 79:2	The dead bodies of thy seruats haue they giuen to be meat vnto foules of ye heauen: and the flesh of thy Saintes vnto the beastes of the earth.
Psalm 79:3	Their blood haue they shead like waters rounde about Ierusalem, and there was none to burie them.
Psalm 79:4	Wee are a reproche to our neighbours, euen a scorne and derision vnto them that are round about vs.
Psalm 79:5	Lord, howe long wilt thou be angrie, for euer? shall thy gelousie burne like fire?
Psalm 79:6	Powre out thy wrath vpon the heathen that haue not knowen thee, and vpon the kingdomes that haue not called vpon thy Name.
Psalm 79:7	For they haue deuoured Iaakob and made his dwelling place desolate.
Psalm 79:8	Remember not against vs the former iniquities, but make haste and let thy tender mercies preuent vs: for we are in great miserie.
Psalm 79:9	Helpe vs, O God of our saluation, for the glorie of thy Name, and deliuer vs, and be mercifull vnto our sinnes for thy Names sake.
Psalm 79:10	Wherefore should the heathen say, Where is their God? let him be knowen among the heathen in our sight by the vengeance of the blood of thy seruants that is shed.
Psalm 79:11	Let the sighing of the prisoners come before thee: according to thy mightie arme preserue the children of death,
Psalm 79:12	And render to our neighbours seuen folde into their bosome their reproche, wherewith they haue reproched thee, O Lord.
Psalm 79:13	So wee thy people, and sheepe of thy pasture shall praise thee for euer: and from generation to generation we will set foorth thy praise.
Psalm 80:1	To him that excelleth on Shoshannim Eduth. A Psalme committed to Asaph. Heare, O thou Shepheard of Israel, thou that leadest Ioseph like sheepe: shewe thy brightnes, thou that sittest betweene the Cherubims.
Psalm 80:2	Before Ephraim and Beniamin and Manasseh stirre vp thy strength, and come to helpe vs.
Psalm 80:3	Turne vs againe, O God, and cause thy face to shine that we may be saued.
Psalm 80:4	O Lord God of hostes, how long wilt thou be angrie against the prayer of thy people?
Psalm 80:5	Thou hast fedde them with the bread of teares, and giuen them teares to drinke with great measure.
Psalm 80:6	Thou hast made vs a strife vnto our neighbours, and our enemies laugh at vs among themselues.
Psalm 80:7	Turne vs againe, O God of hostes: cause thy face to shine, and we shalbe saued.
Psalm 80:8	Thou hast brought a vine out of Egypt: thou hast cast out the heathen, and planted it.
Psalm 80:9	Thou madest roume for it, and didest cause it to take roote, and it filled the land.
Psalm 80:10	The mountaines were couered with the shadowe of it, and the boughes thereof were like the goodly cedars.
Psalm 80:11	Shee stretched out her branches vnto the Sea, and her boughes vnto the Riuer.
Psalm 80:12	Why hast thou then broken downe her hedges, so that all they, which passe by the way, haue plucked her?
Psalm 80:13	The wilde bore out of the wood hath destroyed it, and the wilde beastes of the fielde haue eaten it vp.
Psalm 80:14	Returne we beseech thee, O God of hostes: looke downe from heauen and beholde and visite this vine,
Psalm 80:15	And the vineyard, that thy right hand hath planted, and the young vine, which thou madest strong for thy selfe.
Psalm 80:16	It is burnt with fire and cut downe: and they perish at the rebuke of thy countenance.
Psalm 80:17	Let thine hande be vpon the man of thy right hande, and vpon the sonne of man, whome thou madest strong for thine owne selfe.
Psalm 80:18	So will not we goe backe from thee: reuiue thou vs, and we shall call vpon thy Name.
Psalm 80:19	Turne vs againe, O Lord God of hostes: cause thy face to shine and we shalbe saued.
Psalm 81:1	To him that excelleth upon Gittith. A Psalme committed to Asaph. Sing ioyfully vnto God our strength: sing loude vnto the God of Iaakob.
Psalm 81:2	Take the song and bring forth the timbrel, the pleasant harpe with the viole.
Psalm 81:3	Blowe the trumpet in the newe moone, euen in the time appointed, at our feast day.
Psalm 81:4	For this is a statute for Israel, and a Law of the God of Iaakob.
Psalm 81:5	Hee set this in Ioseph for a testimonie, when hee came out of the land of Egypt, where I heard a language, that I vnderstoode not.
Psalm 81:6	I haue withdrawen his shoulder from the burden, and his handes haue left the pots.
Psalm 81:7	Thou calledst in affliction and I deliuered thee, and answered thee in the secret of the thunder: I prooued thee at the waters of Meribah. Selah.
Psalm 81:8	Heare, O my people, and I wil protest vnto thee: O Israel, if thou wilt hearken vnto me,
Psalm 81:9	Let there bee no strange god in thee, neither worship thou any strange god.
Psalm 81:10	For I am the Lord thy God, which brought thee out of the land of Egypt: open thy mouth wide and I will fill it.
Psalm 81:11	But my people would not heare my voyce, and Israel would none of me.
Psalm 81:12	So I gaue them vp vnto the hardnesse of their heart, and they haue walked in their owne cousels.
Psalm 81:13	Oh that my people had hearkened vnto me, and Israel had walked in my wayes.
Psalm 81:14	I would soone haue humbled their enemies, and turned mine hand against their aduersaries.
Psalm 81:15	The haters of the Lord should haue bene subiect vnto him, and their time should haue endured for euer.
Psalm 81:16	And God would haue fedde them with the fatte of wheat, and with honie out of the rocke would I haue sufficed thee.
Psalm 82:1	A Psalme committed to Aspah. God standeth in the assemblie of gods: hee iudgeth among gods.
Psalm 82:2	How long wil ye iudge vniustly, and accept the persons of the wicked? Selah.
Psalm 82:3	Doe right to the poore and fatherlesse: doe iustice to the poore and needie.
Psalm 82:4	Deliuer the poore and needie: saue them from the hand of the wicked.
Psalm 82:5	They knowe not and vnderstand nothing: they walke in darkenes, albeit all the foundations of the earth be mooued.
Psalm 82:6	I haue said, Ye are gods, and ye all are children of the most High.
Psalm 82:7	But ye shall die as a man, and yee princes, shall fall like others.
Psalm 82:8	O God, arise, therefore iudge thou the earth: for thou shalt inherite all nations.
Psalm 83:1	A song, or Psalme committed to Asaph. Keep not thou silence, O God: bee not still, and cease not, O God.
Psalm 83:2	For lo, thine enemies make a tumult: and they that hate thee, haue lifted vp the head.
Psalm 83:3	They haue taken craftie counsell against thy people, and haue consulted against thy secret ones.
Psalm 83:4	They haue said, Come and let vs cut them off from being a nation: and let the name of Israel be no more in remembrance.
Psalm 83:5	For they haue consulted together in heart, and haue made a league against thee:
Psalm 83:6	The tabernacles of Edom, and the Ishmaelites, Moab and the Agarims:
Psalm 83:7	Gebal and Ammon, and Amalech, the Philistims with the inhabitants of Tyrus:
Psalm 83:8	Asshur also is ioyned with them: they haue bene an arme to the children of Lot. Selah.
Psalm 83:9	Doe thou to them as vnto the Midianites: as to Sisera and as to Iabin at the riuer of Kishon.
Psalm 83:10	They perished at En-dor, and were dung for the earth.
Psalm 83:11	Make them, euen their princes like Oreb and like Zeeb: yea, all their princes like Zebah and like Zalmuna.
Psalm 83:12	Which haue said, Let vs take for our possession the habitations of God.
Psalm 83:13	O my God, make them like vnto a wheele, and as the stubble before the winde.
Psalm 83:14	As the fire burneth the forest, and as the flame setteth the mountaines on fire:
Psalm 83:15	So persecute them with thy tempest, and make them afraide with thy storme.
Psalm 83:16	Fill their faces with shame, that they may seeke thy Name, O Lord.
Psalm 83:17	Let them be confounded and troubled for euer: yea, let them be put to shame and perish,
Psalm 83:18	That they may knowe that thou, which art called Iehouah, art alone, euen the most High ouer all the earth.
Psalm 84:1	To him that excelleth upon Gittith. A Psalme committed to the sonnes of Korah. O Lord of hostes, howe amiable are thy Tabernacles!
Psalm 84:2	My soule longeth, yea, and fainteth for the courtes of the Lord: for mine heart and my flesh reioyce in the liuing God.
Psalm 84:3	Yea, the sparrowe hath found her an house, and the swallow a nest for her, where she may lay her yong: euen by thine altars, O Lord of hostes, my King and my God.
Psalm 84:4	Blessed are they that dwell in thine house: they will euer praise thee. Selah.
Psalm 84:5	Blessed is the man, whose strength is in thee, and in whose heart are thy wayes.
Psalm 84:6	They going through the vale of Baca, make welles therein: the raine also couereth the pooles.
Psalm 84:7	They goe from strength to strength, till euery one appeare before God in Zion.
Psalm 84:8	O Lord God of hostes, heare my prayer: hearken, O God of Iaakob. Selah.
Psalm 84:9	Beholde, O God, our shielde, and looke vpon the face of thine Anointed.
Psalm 84:10	For a day in thy courtes is better then a thousand other where: I had rather be a doore keeper in the House of my God, then to dwell in the Tabernacles of wickednesse.
Psalm 84:11	For the Lord God is the sunne and shielde vnto vs: the Lord will giue grace and glory, and no good thing will he withhold from them that walke vprightly.
Psalm 84:12	O Lord of hostes, blessed is the man that trusteth in thee.
Psalm 85:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme committed to the sonnes of Korah. Lord, thou hast bene fauourable vnto thy land: thou hast brought againe the captiuitie of Iaakob.
Psalm 85:2	Thou hast forgiuen the iniquitie of thy people, and couered all their sinnes. Selah.
Psalm 85:3	Thou hast withdrawen all thine anger, and hast turned backe from the fiercenes of thy wrath.
Psalm 85:4	Turne vs, O God of our saluation, and release thine anger toward vs.
Psalm 85:5	Wilt thou be angry with vs for euer? and wilt thou prolong thy wrath from one generation to another?
Psalm 85:6	Wilt thou not turne againe and quicken vs, that thy people may reioyce in thee?
Psalm 85:7	Shew vs thy mercie, O Lord, and graunt vs thy saluation.
Psalm 85:8	I will hearken what the Lord God will say: for he will speake peace vnto his people, and to his Saintes, that they turne not againe to follie.
Psalm 85:9	Surely his saluation is neere to them that feare him, that glory may dwell in our land.
Psalm 85:10	Mercie and trueth shall meete: righteousnes and peace shall kisse one another.
Psalm 85:11	Trueth shall bud out of the earth, and righteousnes shall looke downe from heauen.
Psalm 85:12	Yea, the Lord shall giue good things, and our land shall giue her increase.
Psalm 85:13	Righteousnesse shall go before him, and shall set her steps in the way.
Psalm 86:1	A prayer of David. Incline thine eare, O Lord, and heare me: for I am poore and needy.
Psalm 86:2	Preserue thou my soule, for I am mercifull: my God, saue thou thy seruant, that trusteth in thee.
Psalm 86:3	Be mercifull vnto me, O Lord: for I crie vpon thee continually.
Psalm 86:4	Reioyce the soule of thy seruant: for vnto thee, O Lord, doe I lift vp my soule.
Psalm 86:5	For thou, Lord, art good and mercifull, and of great kindenes vnto all them, that call vpon thee.
Psalm 86:6	Giue eare, Lord, vnto my prayer, and hearken to the voyce of my supplication.
Psalm 86:7	In the day of my trouble I will call vpon thee: for thou hearest me.
Psalm 86:8	Among the gods there is none like thee, O Lord, and there is none that can doe like thy workes.
Psalm 86:9	All nations, whome thou hast made, shall come and worship before thee, O Lord, and shall glorifie thy Name.
Psalm 86:10	For thou art great and doest wonderous things: thou art God alone.
Psalm 86:11	Teach me thy way, O Lord, and I will walke in thy trueth: knit mine heart vnto thee, that I may feare thy Name.
Psalm 86:12	I wil prayse thee, O Lord my God, with all mine heart: yea, I wil glorifie thy Name for euer.
Psalm 86:13	For great is thy mercie toward me, and thou hast deliuered my soule from the lowest graue.
Psalm 86:14	O God, the proude are risen against me, and the assemblies of violent men haue sought my soule, and haue not set thee before them.
Psalm 86:15	But thou, O Lord, art a pitifull God and mercifull, slowe to anger and great in kindenes and trueth.
Psalm 86:16	Turne vnto me, and haue mercy vpon me: giue thy strength vnto thy seruant, and saue the sonne of thine handmayd.
Psalm 86:17	Shew a token of thy goodnes towarde me, that they which hate me, may see it, and be ashamed, because thou, O Lord, hast holpen me and comforted me.
Psalm 87:1	A Psalme or song committed to the sonnes of Korah. God layde his foundations among the holy mountaines.
Psalm 87:2	The Lord loueth the gates of Zion aboue all the habitations of Iaakob.
Psalm 87:3	Glorious things are spoken of thee, O citie of God. Selah.
Psalm 87:4	I will make mention of Rahab and Babel among them that knowe me: beholde Palestina and Tyrus with Ethiopia, There is he borne.
Psalm 87:5	And of Zion it shall be sayde, Many are borne in her: and he, euen the most High shall stablish her.
Psalm 87:6	The Lord shall count, when hee writeth the people, He was borne there. Selah.
Psalm 87:7	Aswell the singers as the players on instruments shall prayse thee: all my springs are in thee.
Psalm 88:1	A song or Psalme of Heman the Ezrahite to give instruction, committed to the sonnes of Korah for him that excelleth upon Malath Leannoth. O Lord God of my saluation, I cry day and night before thee.
Psalm 88:2	Let my prayer enter into thy presence: incline thine eare vnto my cry.
Psalm 88:3	For my soule is filled with euils, and my life draweth neere to the graue.
Psalm 88:4	I am counted among them that go downe vnto the pit, and am as a man without strength:
Psalm 88:5	Free among the dead, like the slaine lying in the graue, whome thou remembrest no more, and they are cut off from thine hand.
Psalm 88:6	Thou hast layde me in the lowest pit, in darkenes, and in the deepe.
Psalm 88:7	Thine indignation lyeth vpon me, and thou hast vexed me with all thy waues. Selah.
Psalm 88:8	Thou hast put away mine acquaintance farre from me, and made mee to be abhorred of them: I am shut vp, and cannot get foorth.
Psalm 88:9	Mine eye is sorowfull through mine affliction: Lord, I call dayly vpon thee: I stretch out mine hands vnto thee.
Psalm 88:10	Wilt thou shewe a miracle to the dead? or shall the dead rise and prayse thee? Selah.
Psalm 88:11	Shall thy louing kindenes be declared in the graue? or thy faithfulnes in destruction?
Psalm 88:12	Shall thy wonderous workes be knowen in the darke? and thy righteousnes in the land of obliuion?
Psalm 88:13	But vnto thee haue I cryed, O Lord, and early shall my prayer come before thee.
Psalm 88:14	Lord, why doest thou reiect my soule, and hidest thy face from me?
Psalm 88:15	I am afflicted and at the point of death: from my youth I suffer thy terrours, doubting of my life.
Psalm 88:16	Thine indignations goe ouer me, and thy feare hath cut me off.
Psalm 88:17	They came round about me dayly like water, and compassed me together.
Psalm 88:18	My louers and friends hast thou put away from me, and mine acquaintance hid themselues.
Psalm 89:1	A Psalme to give instruction, of Ethan the Ezrahite. I will sing the mercies of the Lord for euer: with my mouth will I declare thy trueth from generation to generation.
Psalm 89:2	For I said, Mercie shalbe set vp for euer: thy trueth shalt thou stablish in ye very heauens.
Psalm 89:3	I haue made a couenant with my chosen: I haue sworne to Dauid my seruant,
Psalm 89:4	Thy seede will I stablish for euer, and set vp thy throne from generation to generation. Selah.
Psalm 89:5	O Lord, euen the heauens shall prayse thy wonderous worke: yea, thy trueth in the Congregation of the Saints.
Psalm 89:6	For who is equall to the Lord in the heauen? and who is like the Lord among the sonnes of the gods?
Psalm 89:7	God is very terrible in the assemblie of the Saints, and to be reuerenced aboue all, that are about him.
Psalm 89:8	O Lord God of hostes, who is like vnto thee, which art a mightie Lord, and thy trueth is about thee?
Psalm 89:9	Thou rulest the raging of the sea: when the waues thereof arise, thou stillest them.
Psalm 89:10	Thou hast beaten downe Rahab as a man slaine: thou hast scattered thine enemies with thy mightie arme.
Psalm 89:11	The heauens are thine, the earth also is thine: thou hast layde the foundation of the world, and all that therein is.
Psalm 89:12	Thou hast created the North and the South: Tabor and Hermon shall reioyce in thy Name.
Psalm 89:13	Thou hast a mightie arme: strong is thine hand, and high is thy right hand.
Psalm 89:14	Righteousnesse and equitie are the stablishment of thy throne: mercy and trueth goe before thy face.
Psalm 89:15	Blessed is the people, that can reioyce in thee: they shall walke in the light of thy countenance, O Lord.
Psalm 89:16	They shall reioyce continually in thy Name, and in thy righteousnes shall they exalt them selues.
Psalm 89:17	For thou art the glory of their strength, and by thy fauour our hornes shall be exalted.
Psalm 89:18	For our shield apperteineth to the Lord, and our King to the holy one of Israel.
Psalm 89:19	Thou spakest then in a vision vnto thine Holy one, and saydest, I haue layde helpe vpon one that is mightie: I haue exalted one chosen out of the people.
Psalm 89:20	I haue found Dauid my seruant: with mine holy oyle haue I anoynted him.
Psalm 89:21	Therefore mine hande shall be established with him, and mine arme shall strengthen him.
Psalm 89:22	The enemie shall not oppresse him, neither shall the wicked hurt him.
Psalm 89:23	But I will destroy his foes before his face, and plague them that hate him.
Psalm 89:24	My trueth also and my mercie shall be with him, and in my Name shall his horne be exalted.
Psalm 89:25	I will set his hand also in the sea, and his right hand in the floods.
Psalm 89:26	He shall cry vnto mee, Thou art my Father, my God and the rocke of my saluation.
Psalm 89:27	Also I wil make him my first borne, higher then the Kings of the earth.
Psalm 89:28	My mercie will I keepe for him for euermore, and my couenant shall stande fast with him.
Psalm 89:29	His seede also will I make to endure for euer, and his throne as the dayes of heauen.
Psalm 89:30	But if his children forsake my Lawe, and walke not in my iudgements:
Psalm 89:31	If they breake my statutes, and keepe not my commandements:
Psalm 89:32	Then will I visite their transgression with the rod, and their iniquitie with strokes.
Psalm 89:33	Yet my louing kindnesse will I not take from him, neither will I falsifie my trueth.
Psalm 89:34	My couenant wil I not breake, nor alter the thing that is gone out of my lips.
Psalm 89:35	I haue sworne once by mine holines, that I will not fayle Dauid, saying,
Psalm 89:36	His seede shall endure for euer, and his throne shalbe as the sunne before me.
Psalm 89:37	He shalbe established for euermore as the moone, and as a faythfull witnes in the heauen. Selah.
Psalm 89:38	But thou hast reiected and abhorred, thou hast bene angry with thine Anoynted.
Psalm 89:39	Thou hast broken the couenant of thy seruant, and profaned his crowne, casting it on the ground.
Psalm 89:40	Thou hast broken downe all his walles: thou hast layd his fortresses in ruine.
Psalm 89:41	All that goe by the way, spoyle him: he is a rebuke vnto his neighbours.
Psalm 89:42	Thou hast set vp the right hand of his enemies, and made all his aduersaries to reioyce.
Psalm 89:43	Thou hast also turned the edge of his sworde, and hast not made him to stand in the battell.
Psalm 89:44	Thou hast caused his dignitie to decay, and cast his throne to the ground.
Psalm 89:45	The dayes of his youth hast thou shortned, and couered him with shame. Selah.
Psalm 89:46	Lord, howe long wilt thou hide thy selfe, for euer? shall thy wrath burne like fire?
Psalm 89:47	Remember of what time I am: wherefore shouldest thou create in vaine all the children of men?
Psalm 89:48	What man liueth, and shall not see death? shall hee deliuer his soule from the hande of the graue? Selah.
Psalm 89:49	Lord, where are thy former mercies, which thou swarest vnto Dauid in thy trueth?
Psalm 89:50	Remember, O Lord, the rebuke of thy seruants, which I beare in my bosome of all the mightie people.
Psalm 89:51	For thine enemies haue reproched thee, O Lord, because they haue reproched the footesteps of thine Anointed.
Psalm 89:52	Praised be the Lord for euermore. So be it, euen so be it.
Psalm 90:1	A prayer of Moses, the man of God. Lord, thou hast bene our habitation from generation to generation.
Psalm 90:2	Before the mountaines were made, and before thou hadst formed the earth, and the world, euen from euerlasting to euerlasting thou art our God.
Psalm 90:3	Thou turnest man to destruction: againe thou sayest, Returne, ye sonnes of Adam.
Psalm 90:4	For a thousande yeeres in thy sight are as yesterday when it is past, and as a watch in the night.
Psalm 90:5	Thou hast ouerflowed them: they are as a sleepe: in the morning he groweth like the grasse:
Psalm 90:6	In the morning it florisheth and groweth, but in the euening it is cut downe and withereth.
Psalm 90:7	For we are consumed by thine anger, and by thy wrath are we troubled.
Psalm 90:8	Thou hast set our iniquities before thee, and our secret sinnes in the light of thy countenance.
Psalm 90:9	For all our dayes are past in thine anger: we haue spent our yeeres as a thought.
Psalm 90:10	The time of our life is threescore yeeres and ten, and if they be of strength, fourescore yeeres: yet their strength is but labour and sorowe: for it is cut off quickly, and we flee away.
Psalm 90:11	Who knoweth the power of thy wrath? for according to thy feare is thine anger.
Psalm 90:12	Teach vs so to nomber our dayes, that we may apply our heartes vnto wisdome.
Psalm 90:13	Returne (O Lord, howe long?) and be pacified toward thy seruants.
Psalm 90:14	Fill vs with thy mercie in the morning: so shall we reioyce and be glad all our dayes.
Psalm 90:15	Comfort vs according to the dayes that thou hast afflicted vs, and according to the yeeres that we haue seene euill.
Psalm 90:16	Let thy worke bee seene towarde thy seruants, and thy glory vpon their children.
Psalm 90:17	And let the beautie of the Lord our God be vpon vs, and direct thou the worke of our hands vpon vs, euen direct the worke of our handes.
Psalm 91:1	Who so dwelleth in the secrete of the most High, shall abide in the shadowe of the Almightie.
Psalm 91:2	I will say vnto the Lord, O mine hope, and my fortresse: he is my God, in him will I trust.
Psalm 91:3	Surely he will deliuer thee from the snare of the hunter, and from the noysome pestilence.
Psalm 91:4	Hee will couer thee vnder his winges, and thou shalt be sure vnder his feathers: his trueth shall be thy shielde and buckler.
Psalm 91:5	Thou shalt not be afraide of the feare of the night, nor of the arrowe that flyeth by day:
Psalm 91:6	Nor of the pestilence that walketh in the darkenesse: nor of the plague that destroyeth at noone day.
Psalm 91:7	A thousand shall fall at thy side, and tenne thousand at thy right hand, but it shall not come neere thee.
Psalm 91:8	Doubtlesse with thine eyes shalt thou beholde and see the reward of the wicked.
Psalm 91:9	For thou hast said, The Lord is mine hope: thou hast set the most High for thy refuge.
Psalm 91:10	There shall none euill come vnto thee, neither shall any plague come neere thy tabernacle.
Psalm 91:11	For hee shall giue his Angels charge ouer thee to keepe thee in all thy wayes.
Psalm 91:12	They shall beare thee in their handes, that thou hurt not thy foote against a stone.
Psalm 91:13	Thou shalt walke vpon the lyon and aspe: the yong lyon and the dragon shalt thou treade vnder feete.
Psalm 91:14	Because he hath loued me, therefore will I deliuer him: I will exalt him because hee hath knowen my Name.
Psalm 91:15	He shall call vpon me, and I wil heare him: I will be with him in trouble: I will deliuer him, and glorifie him.
Psalm 91:16	With long life wil I satisfie him, and shew him my saluation.
Psalm 92:1	A Psalme or song for the Sabbath day. It is a good thing to praise the Lord, and to sing vnto thy Name, O most High,
Psalm 92:2	To declare thy louing kindenesse in the morning, and thy trueth in the night,
Psalm 92:3	Vpon an instrument of tenne strings, and vpon the viole with the song vpon the harpe.
Psalm 92:4	For thou, Lord, hast made mee glad by thy workes, and I wil reioyce in the workes of thine handes.
Psalm 92:5	O Lord, how glorious are thy workes! and thy thoughtes are very deepe.
Psalm 92:6	An vnwise man knoweth it not, and a foole doeth not vnderstand this,
Psalm 92:7	(When the wicked growe as the grasse, and all the workers of wickednesse doe flourish) that they shall be destroyed for euer.
Psalm 92:8	But thou, O Lord, art most High for euermore.
Psalm 92:9	For loe, thine enemies, O Lord: for loe, thine enemies shall perish: all the workers of iniquitie shall be destroyed.
Psalm 92:10	But thou shalt exalt mine horne, like the vnicornes, and I shalbe anoynted with fresh oyle.
Psalm 92:11	Mine eye also shall see my desire against mine enemies: and mine eares shall heare my wish against the wicked, that rise vp against me.
Psalm 92:12	The righteous shall flourish like a palme tree, and shall grow like a Cedar in Lebanon.
Psalm 92:13	Such as bee planted in the house of the Lord, shall flourish in the courtes of our God.
Psalm 92:14	They shall still bring foorth fruite in their age: they shall be fat and flourishing,
Psalm 92:15	To declare that the Lord my rocke is righteous, and that none iniquitie is in him.
Psalm 93:1	The Lord reigneth, and is clothed with maiestie: the Lord is clothed, and girded with power: the world also shall be established, that it cannot be mooued.
Psalm 93:2	Thy throne is established of olde: thou art from euerlasting.
Psalm 93:3	The floodes haue lifted vp, O Lord: the floodes haue lifted vp their voyce: the floods lift vp their waues.
Psalm 93:4	The waues of ye sea are marueilous through the noyse of many waters, yet the Lord on High is more mightie.
Psalm 93:5	Thy testimonies are very sure: holinesse becommeth thine House, O Lord, for euer.
Psalm 94:1	O Lord God the auenger, O God the auenger, shewe thy selfe clearely.
Psalm 94:2	Exalt thy selfe, O Iudge of the worlde, and render a reward to the proude.
Psalm 94:3	Lord how long shall the wicked, how long shall the wicked triumph?
Psalm 94:4	They prate and speake fiercely: all the workers of iniquitie vaunt themselues.
Psalm 94:5	They smite downe thy people, O Lord, and trouble thine heritage.
Psalm 94:6	They slay the widowe and the stranger, and murder the fatherlesse.
Psalm 94:7	Yet they say, The Lord shall not see: neither will the God of Iaakob regard it.
Psalm 94:8	Vnderstande ye vnwise among the people: and ye fooles, when will ye be wise?
Psalm 94:9	Hee that planted the eare, shall hee not heare? or he that formed the eye, shall he not see?
Psalm 94:10	Or he that chastiseth the nations, shall he not correct? hee that teacheth man knowledge, shall he not knowe?
Psalm 94:11	The Lord knoweth the thoughtes of man, that they are vanitie.
Psalm 94:12	Blessed is the man, whom thou chastisest, O Lord, and teachest him in thy Lawe,
Psalm 94:13	That thou mayest giue him rest from the dayes of euill, whiles the pitte is digged for the wicked.
Psalm 94:14	Surely the Lord will not faile his people, neither will he forsake his inheritance.
Psalm 94:15	For iudgement shall returne to iustice, and all the vpright in heart shall follow after it.
Psalm 94:16	Who will rise vp with me against the wicked? or who will take my part against the workers of iniquitie?
Psalm 94:17	If the Lord had not holpen me, my soule had almost dwelt in silence.
Psalm 94:18	When I said, My foote slideth, thy mercy, O Lord, stayed me.
Psalm 94:19	In the multitude of my thoughts in mine heart, thy comfortes haue reioyced my soule.
Psalm 94:20	Hath the throne of iniquitie fellowship with thee, which forgeth wrong for a Lawe?
Psalm 94:21	They gather them together against the soule of the righteous, and condemne the innocent blood.
Psalm 94:22	But the Lord is my refuge, and my God is the rocke of mine hope.
Psalm 94:23	And hee will recompence them their wickednes, and destroy them in their owne malice: yea, the Lord our God shall destroy them.
Psalm 95:1	Come, let vs reioyce vnto the Lord: let vs sing aloude vnto the rocke of our saluation.
Psalm 95:2	Let vs come before his face with praise: let vs sing loude vnto him with Psalmes.
Psalm 95:3	For the Lord is a great God, and a great King aboue all gods.
Psalm 95:4	In whose hande are the deepe places of the earth, and the heightes of the mountaines are his:
Psalm 95:5	To whome the Sea belongeth: for hee made it, and his handes formed the dry land.
Psalm 95:6	Come, let vs worship and fall downe, and kneele before the Lord our maker.
Psalm 95:7	For he is our God, and we are the people of his pasture, and the sheepe of his hande: to day, if ye will heare his voyce,
Psalm 95:8	Harden not your heart, as in Meribah, and as in the day of Massah in the wildernesse.
Psalm 95:9	Where your fathers tempted me, proued me, though they had seene my worke.
Psalm 95:10	Fourtie yeeres haue I contended with this generation, and said, They are a people that erre in heart, for they haue not knowen my wayes.
Psalm 95:11	Wherefore I sware in my wrath, saying, Surely they shall not enter into my rest.
Psalm 96:1	Sing vnto the Lord a newe song: sing vnto the Lord, all the earth.
Psalm 96:2	Sing vnto the Lord, and prayse his Name: declare his saluation from day to day.
Psalm 96:3	Declare his glory among all nations, and his wonders among all people.
Psalm 96:4	For the Lord is great and much to be praysed: he is to be feared aboue all gods.
Psalm 96:5	For all the gods of the people are idoles: but the Lord made the heauens.
Psalm 96:6	Strength and glory are before him: power and beautie are in his Sanctuarie.
Psalm 96:7	Giue vnto the Lord, ye families of the people: giue vnto the Lord glory and power.
Psalm 96:8	Giue vnto the Lord the glory of his Name: bring an offering, and enter into his courtes.
Psalm 96:9	Worship the Lord in the glorious Sanctuarie: tremble before him all the earth.
Psalm 96:10	Say among the nations, The Lord reigneth: surely the world shalbe stable, and not moue, and he shall iudge the people in righteousnesse.
Psalm 96:11	Let the heauens reioyce, and let the earth be glad: let the sea roare, and all that therein is.
Psalm 96:12	Let the field be ioyfull, and all that is in it: let all the trees of the wood then reioyce
Psalm 96:13	Before the Lord: for he commeth, for he cometh to iudge the earth: he wil iudge the world with righteousnes, and the people in his trueth.
Psalm 97:1	The Lord reigneth: let the earth reioyce: let the multitude of the yles be glad.
Psalm 97:2	Cloudes and darkenes are round about him: righteousnesse and iudgement are the foundation of his throne.
Psalm 97:3	There shall goe a fire before him, and burne vp his enemies round about.
Psalm 97:4	His lightnings gaue light vnto the worlde: the earth sawe it and was afraide.
Psalm 97:5	The mountaines melted like waxe at the presence of the Lord, at the presence of the Lord of the whole earth.
Psalm 97:6	The heauens declare his righteousnes, and all the people see his glory.
Psalm 97:7	Confounded be all they that serue grauen images, and that glory in idoles: worship him all ye gods.
Psalm 97:8	Zion heard of it, and was glad: and the daughters of Iudah reioyced, because of thy iudgements, O Lord.
Psalm 97:9	For thou, Lord, art most High aboue all the earth: thou art much exalted aboue all gods.
Psalm 97:10	Ye that loue the Lord, hate euill: he preserueth the soules of his Saints: hee will deliuer them from the hand of the wicked.
Psalm 97:11	Light is sowen for the righteous, and ioy for the vpright in heart.
Psalm 97:12	Reioyce ye righteous in the Lord, and giue thankes for his holy remembrance.

Psalm 98:2	The Lord declared his saluation: his righteousnes hath he reueiled in the sight of ye nations.
Psalm 98:3	He hath remembred his mercy and his trueth toward the house of Israel: all the ends of the earth haue seene the saluation of our God.
Psalm 98:4	All the earth, sing ye loude vnto the Lord: crie out and reioyce, and sing prayses.

Psalm 99:2	The Lord is great in Zion, and he is high aboue all the people.
Psalm 99:3	They shall prayse thy great and fearefull Name (for it is holy)
Psalm 99:4	And the Kings power, that loueth iudgement: for thou hast prepared equitie: thou hast executed iudgement and iustice in Iaakob.

Psalm 100:1	A Psalme of Praise. Sing ye loude vnto the Lord, all the earth.
Psalm 100:2	Serue the Lord with gladnes: come before him with ioyfulnes.

Psalm 100:4	Enter into his gates with prayse, and into his courts with reioycing: prayse him and blesse his Name.
Psalm 100:5	For the Lord is good: his mercy is euerlasting, and his trueth is from generation to generation.
Psalm 101:1	A Psalme of David. I will sing mercie and iudgement: vnto thee, O Lord, will I sing.
Psalm 101:2	I will doe wisely in the perfite way, till thou commest to me: I will walke in the vprightnes of mine heart in the middes of mine house.
Psalm 101:3	I wil set no wicked thing before mine eyes: I hate the worke of them that fall away: it shall not cleaue vnto me.
Psalm 101:4	A froward heart shall depart from me: I will knowe none euill.
Psalm 101:5	Him that priuily slandereth his neighbour, wil I destroy: him that hath a proude looke and hie heart, I cannot suffer.
Psalm 101:6	Mine eyes shalbe vnto the faithfull of the lande, that they may dwell with me: he that walketh in a perfite way, he shall serue me.
Psalm 101:7	There shall no deceitful person dwell within mine house: he that telleth lyes, shall not remaine in my sight.
Psalm 101:8	Betimes will I destroy all the wicked of the land, that I may cut off all the workers of iniquitie from the Citie of the Lord.
Psalm 102:1	A prayer of the afflicted, when he shall be in distresse, and pour forth his meditation before the Lord. O Lord, heare my prayer, and let my crye come vnto thee.
Psalm 102:2	Hide not thy face from me in the time of my trouble: incline thine eares vnto me: when I call, make haste to heare me.
Psalm 102:3	For my dayes are consumed like smoke, and my bones are burnt like an herthe.
Psalm 102:4	Mine heart is smitten and withereth like grasse, because I forgate to eate my bread.
Psalm 102:5	For the voyce of my groning my bones doe cleaue to my skinne.
Psalm 102:6	I am like a pelicane of the wildernesse: I am like an owle of the deserts.
Psalm 102:7	I watch and am as a sparrowe alone vpon the house top.
Psalm 102:8	Mine enemies reuile me dayly, and they that rage against me, haue sworne against me.
Psalm 102:9	Surely I haue eaten asshes as bread, and mingled my drinke with weeping,
Psalm 102:10	Because of thine indignation and thy wrath: for thou hast heaued me vp, and cast me downe.
Psalm 102:11	My dayes are like a shadowe that fadeth, and I am withered like grasse.
Psalm 102:12	But thou, O Lord, doest remaine for euer, and thy remembrance from generation to generation.
Psalm 102:13	Thou wilt arise and haue mercy vpon Zion: for the time to haue mercie thereon, for the appointed time is come.
Psalm 102:14	For thy seruants delite in the stones thereof, and haue pitie on the dust thereof.
Psalm 102:15	Then the heathen shall feare the Name of the Lord, and all the Kings of the earth thy glory,
Psalm 102:16	When the Lord shall build vp Zion, and shall appeare in his glory,
Psalm 102:17	And shall turne vnto the prayer of the desolate, and not despise their prayer.
Psalm 102:18	This shall be written for the generation to come: and the people, which shalbe created, shall prayse the Lord.
Psalm 102:19	For he hath looked downe from the height of his Sanctuarie: out of the heauen did the Lord beholde the earth,
Psalm 102:20	That he might heare the mourning of the prisoner, and deliuer the children of death:
Psalm 102:21	That they may declare the Name of the Lord in Zion, and his prayse in Ierusalem,
Psalm 102:22	When the people shalbe gathered together, and the kingdomes to serue the Lord.
Psalm 102:23	He abated my strength in the way, and shortened my dayes.
Psalm 102:24	And I sayd, O my God, take me not away in the middes of my dayes: thy yeeres endure from generation to generation.
Psalm 102:25	Thou hast aforetime layde the foundation of the earth, and the heauens are the worke of thine hands.
Psalm 102:26	They shall perish, but thou shalt endure: euen they all shall waxe olde as doeth a garment: as a vesture shalt thou change them, and they shall be changed.
Psalm 102:27	But thou art the same, and thy yeeres shall not fayle.
Psalm 102:28	The children of thy seruants shall continue, and their seede shall stand fast in thy sight.
Psalm 103:1	A Psalme of David. My soule, prayse thou the Lord, and all that is within me, prayse his holy Name.
Psalm 103:2	My soule, prayse thou the Lord, and forget not all his benefites.
Psalm 103:3	Which forgiueth all thine iniquitie, and healeth all thine infirmities.
Psalm 103:4	Which redeemeth thy life from the graue, and crowneth thee with mercy and compassions.
Psalm 103:5	Which satisfieth thy mouth with good things: and thy youth is renued like the eagles.
Psalm 103:6	The Lord executeth righteousnes and iudgement to all that are oppressed.
Psalm 103:7	He made his wayes knowen vnto Moses, and his workes vnto the children of Israel.
Psalm 103:8	The Lord is full of compassion and mercie, slowe to anger and of great kindnesse.
Psalm 103:9	He will not alway chide, neither keepe his anger for euer.
Psalm 103:10	He hath not dealt with vs after our sinnes, nor rewarded vs according to our iniquities.
Psalm 103:11	For as high as the heauen is aboue ye earth, so great is his mercie toward them that feare him.
Psalm 103:12	As farre as the East is from the West: so farre hath he remooued our sinnes from vs.
Psalm 103:13	As a father hath compassion on his children, so hath the Lord compassion on them that feare him.
Psalm 103:14	For he knoweth whereof we be made: he remembreth that we are but dust.
Psalm 103:15	The dayes of man are as grasse: as a flowre of the fielde, so florisheth he.
Psalm 103:16	For the winde goeth ouer it, and it is gone, and the place thereof shall knowe it no more.
Psalm 103:17	But the louing kindnesse of the Lord endureth for euer and euer vpon them that feare him, and his righteousnes vpon childrens children,
Psalm 103:18	Vnto them that keepe his couenant, and thinke vpon his commandements to doe them.
Psalm 103:19	The Lord hath prepared his throne in heauen, and his Kingdome ruleth ouer all.
Psalm 103:20	Prayse the Lord, ye his Angels, that excell in strength, that doe his commandement in obeying the voyce of his worde.
Psalm 103:21	Prayse the Lord, all ye his hostes, ye his seruants that doe his pleasure.
Psalm 103:22	Prayse the Lord, all ye his workes, in all places of his dominion: my soule, prayse thou the Lord.
Psalm 104:1	My soule, prayse thou the Lord: O Lord my God, thou art exceeding great, thou art clothed with glorie and honour.
Psalm 104:2	Which couereth himselfe with light as with a garment, and spreadeth the heauens like a curtaine.
Psalm 104:3	Which layeth the beames of his chambers in the waters, and maketh the cloudes his chariot, and walketh vpon the wings of the winde.
Psalm 104:4	Which maketh his spirits his messengers, and a flaming fire his ministers.
Psalm 104:5	He set the earth vpon her foundations, so that it shall neuer moue.
Psalm 104:6	Thou coueredst it with the deepe as with a garment: the waters woulde stand aboue the mountaines.
Psalm 104:7	But at thy rebuke they flee: at the voyce of thy thunder they haste away.
Psalm 104:8	And the mountaines ascend, and the valleis descend to the place which thou hast established for them.
Psalm 104:9	But thou hast set them a bounde, which they shall not passe: they shall not returne to couer the earth.
Psalm 104:10	He sendeth the springs into the valleis, which runne betweene the mountaines.
Psalm 104:11	They shall giue drinke to all the beasts of the fielde, and the wilde asses shall quench their thirst.
Psalm 104:12	By these springs shall the foules of the heauen dwell, and sing among the branches.
Psalm 104:13	He watereth the mountaines from his chambers, and the earth is filled with the fruite of thy workes.
Psalm 104:14	He causeth grasse to growe for the cattell, and herbe for the vse of man, that he may bring forth bread out of the earth,
Psalm 104:15	And wine that maketh glad the heart of man, and oyle to make the face to shine, and bread that strengtheneth mans heart.
Psalm 104:16	The high trees are satisfied, euen the cedars of Lebanon, which he hath planted,
Psalm 104:17	That ye birdes may make their nestes there: the storke dwelleth in the firre trees.
Psalm 104:18	The high mountaines are for the goates: the rockes are a refuge for the conies.
Psalm 104:19	He appoynted the moone for certaine seasons: the sunne knoweth his going downe.
Psalm 104:20	Thou makest darkenesse, and it is night, wherein all the beastes of the forest creepe forth.
Psalm 104:21	The lions roare after their praye, and seeke their meate at God.
Psalm 104:22	When the sunne riseth, they retire, and couche in their dennes.
Psalm 104:23	Then goeth man forth to his worke, and to his labour vntill the euening.
Psalm 104:24	O Lord, howe manifolde are thy workes! in wisdome hast thou made them all: the earth is full of thy riches.
Psalm 104:25	So is this sea great and wide: for therein are things creeping innumerable, both small beastes and great.
Psalm 104:26	There goe the shippes, yea, that Liuiathan, whom thou hast made to play therein.
Psalm 104:27	All these waite vpon thee, that thou maiest giue them foode in due season.
Psalm 104:28	Thou giuest it to them, and they gather it: thou openest thine hand, and they are filled with good things.
Psalm 104:29	But if thou hide thy face, they are troubled: if thou take away their breath, they dye and returne to their dust.
Psalm 104:30	Againe if thou send forth thy spirit, they are created, and thou renuest the face of the earth.
Psalm 104:31	Glory be to the Lord for euer: let the Lord reioyce in his workes.
Psalm 104:32	He looketh on the earth and it trembleth: he toucheth the mountaines, and they smoke.
Psalm 104:33	I will sing vnto the Lord all my life: I will prayse my God, while I liue.
Psalm 104:34	Let my wordes be acceptable vnto him: I will reioyce in the Lord.
Psalm 104:35	Let the sinners be consumed out of the earth, and the wicked till there be no more: O my soule, prayse thou the Lord. Prayse ye the Lord.
Psalm 105:1	Praise the Lord, and call vpon his Name: declare his workes among the people.
Psalm 105:2	Sing vnto him, sing prayse vnto him, and talke of all his wonderous workes.
Psalm 105:3	Reioyce in his holy Name: let the heart of them that seeke the Lord, reioyce.
Psalm 105:4	Seeke the Lord and his strength: seeke his face continually.
Psalm 105:5	Remember his marueilous woorkes, that he hath done, his wonders and the iudgements of his mouth,
Psalm 105:6	Ye seede of Abraham his seruant, ye children of Iaakob, which are his elect.
Psalm 105:7	He is the Lord our God: his iudgements are through all the earth.
Psalm 105:8	He hath alway remembred his couenant and promise, that he made to a thousand generations,
Psalm 105:9	Euen that which he made with Abraham, and his othe vnto Izhak:
Psalm 105:10	And since hath confirmed it to Iaakob for a lawe, and to Israel for an euerlasting couenant,
Psalm 105:11	Saying, Vnto thee will I giue the land of Canaan, the lot of your inheritance.
Psalm 105:12	Albeit they were fewe in nomber, yea, very fewe, and strangers in the land,
Psalm 105:13	And walked about from nation to nation, from one kingdome to another people,
Psalm 105:14	Yet suffered he no man to doe them wrong, but reprooued Kings for their sakes, saying,
Psalm 105:15	Touche not mine anointed, and doe my Prophets no harme.
Psalm 105:16	Moreouer, he called a famine vpon ye land, and vtterly brake the staffe of bread.
Psalm 105:17	But he sent a man before them: Ioseph was solde for a slaue.
Psalm 105:18	They helde his feete in the stockes, and he was laide in yrons,
Psalm 105:19	Vntill his appointed time came, and the counsell of the Lord had tryed him.
Psalm 105:20	The King sent and loosed him: euen the Ruler of the people deliuered him.
Psalm 105:21	He made him lord of his house, and ruler of all his substance,
Psalm 105:22	That he shoulde binde his princes vnto his will, and teach his Ancients wisedome.
Psalm 105:23	Then Israel came to Egypt, and Iaakob was a stranger in the land of Ham.
Psalm 105:24	And he increased his people exceedingly, and made them stronger then their oppressours.
Psalm 105:25	He turned their heart to hate his people, and to deale craftily with his seruants.
Psalm 105:26	Then sent he Moses his seruant, and Aaron whom he had chosen.
Psalm 105:27	They shewed among them the message of his signes, and wonders in the land of Ham.
Psalm 105:28	He sent darkenesse, and made it darke: and they were not disobedient vnto his commission.
Psalm 105:29	He turned their waters into blood, and slewe their fish.
Psalm 105:30	Their land brought foorth frogs, euen in their Kings chambers.
Psalm 105:31	He spake, and there came swarmes of flies and lice in all their quarters.
Psalm 105:32	He gaue them haile for raine, and flames of fire in their land.
Psalm 105:33	He smote their vines also and their figge trees, and brake downe the trees in their coastes.
Psalm 105:34	He spake, and the grashoppers came, and caterpillers innumerable,
Psalm 105:35	And did eate vp all the grasse in their land, and deuoured the fruite of their ground.
Psalm 105:36	He smote also all the first borne in their land, euen the beginning of all their strength.
Psalm 105:37	He brought them forth also with siluer and golde, and there was none feeble among their tribes.
Psalm 105:38	Egypt was glad at their departing: for the feare of them had fallen vpon them.
Psalm 105:39	He spred a cloude to be a couering, and fire to giue light in the night.
Psalm 105:40	They asked, and he brought quailes, and he filled them with the bread of heauen.
Psalm 105:41	He opened the rocke, and the waters flowed out, and ranne in the drye places like a riuer.
Psalm 105:42	For he remembred his holy promise to Abraham his seruant,
Psalm 105:43	And he brought forth his people with ioy, and his chosen with gladnesse,
Psalm 105:44	And gaue them the lands of the heathen, and they tooke the labours of the people in possession,
Psalm 105:45	That they might keepe his statutes, and obserue his Lawes. Prayse ye the Lord.
Psalm 106:1	Praise ye the Lord. Praise ye the Lord because he is good, for his mercie endureth for euer.
Psalm 106:2	Who can expresse the noble actes of the Lord, or shewe forth all his prayse?
Psalm 106:3	Blessed are they that keepe iudgement, and doe righteousnesse at all times.
Psalm 106:4	Remember me, O Lord, with the fauour of thy people: visite me with thy saluation,
Psalm 106:5	That I may see the felicitie of thy chosen, and reioyce in the ioy of thy people, and glorie with thine inheritance.
Psalm 106:6	We haue sinned with our fathers: we haue committed iniquitie, and done wickedly.
Psalm 106:7	Our fathers vnderstoode not thy wonders in Egypt, neither remembred they the multitude of thy mercies, but rebelled at the Sea, euen at the red sea.
Psalm 106:8	Neuerthelesse he saued them for his Names sake, that he might make his power to be knowen.
Psalm 106:9	And he rebuked the red Sea, and it was dryed vp, and he led them in the deepe, as in the wildernesse.
Psalm 106:10	And he saued them from ye aduersaries hand, and deliuered them from ye hand of the enemie.
Psalm 106:11	And the waters couered their oppressours: not one of them was left.
Psalm 106:12	Then beleeued they his wordes, and sang prayse vnto him.
Psalm 106:13	But incontinently they forgate his workes: they wayted not for his counsell,
Psalm 106:14	But lusted with concupiscence in the wildernes, and tempted God in the desert.
Psalm 106:15	Then he gaue them their desire: but he sent leannesse into their soule.
Psalm 106:16	They enuied Moses also in the tentes, and Aaron the holy one of the Lord.
Psalm 106:17	Therefore the earth opened and swallowed vp Dathan, and couered the companie of Abiram.
Psalm 106:18	And the fire was kindled in their assembly: the flame burnt vp the wicked.
Psalm 106:19	They made a calfe in Horeb, and worshipped the molten image.
Psalm 106:20	Thus they turned their glory into the similitude of a bullocke, that eateth grasse.
Psalm 106:21	They forgate God their Sauiour, which had done great things in Egypt,
Psalm 106:22	Wonderous woorkes in the lande of Ham, and fearefull things by the red Sea.
Psalm 106:23	Therefore he minded to destroy them, had not Moses his chosen stand in the breach before him to turne away his wrath, least he shoulde destroy them.
Psalm 106:24	Also they contemned that pleasant land, and beleeued not his worde,
Psalm 106:25	But murmured in their tentes, and hearkened not vnto the voice of the Lord.
Psalm 106:26	Therefore hee lifted vp his hande against them, to destroy them in the wildernesse,
Psalm 106:27	And to destroy their seede among the nations, and to scatter them throughout the countries.
Psalm 106:28	They ioyned themselues also vnto Baalpeor, and did eate the offrings of the dead.
Psalm 106:29	Thus they prouoked him vnto anger with their owne inuentions, and the plague brake in vpon them.
Psalm 106:30	But Phinehas stoode vp, and executed iudgement, and the plague was staied.
Psalm 106:31	And it was imputed vnto him for righteousnes from generation to generation for euer.
Psalm 106:32	They angred him also at the waters of Meribah, so that Moses was punished for their sakes,
Psalm 106:33	Because they vexed his spirite, so that hee spake vnaduisedly with his lippes.
Psalm 106:34	Neither destroied they the people, as the Lord had commanded them,
Psalm 106:35	But were mingled among the heathen, and learned their workes,
Psalm 106:36	And serued their idoles, which were their ruine.
Psalm 106:37	Yea, they offered their sonnes, and their daughters vnto deuils,
Psalm 106:38	And shed innocent blood, euen the blood of their sonnes, and of their daughters, whome they offred vnto the idoles of Canaan, and the lande was defiled with blood.
Psalm 106:39	Thus were they steined with their owne woorkes, and went a whoring with their owne inuentions.
Psalm 106:40	Therefore was the wrath of the Lord kindled against his people, and he abhorred his owne inheritance.
Psalm 106:41	And hee gaue them into the hande of the heathen: and they that hated them, were lordes ouer them.
Psalm 106:42	Their enemies also oppressed them, and they were humbled vnder their hand.
Psalm 106:43	Many a time did hee deliuer them, but they prouoked him by their counsels: therefore they were brought downe by their iniquitie.
Psalm 106:44	Yet hee sawe when they were in affliction, and he heard their crie.
Psalm 106:45	And he remembred his couenant towarde them and repented according to the multitude of his mercies,
Psalm 106:46	And gaue them fauour in the sight of all them that lead them captiues.
Psalm 106:47	Saue vs, O Lord our God, and gather vs from among the heathen, that we may praise thine holy Name, and glorie in thy praise.
Psalm 106:48	Blessed be the Lord God of Israel for euer and euer, and let all the people say, So be it. Praise yee the Lord.
Psalm 107:1	Praise the Lord, because he is good: for his mercie endureth for euer.
Psalm 107:2	Let them, which haue bene redeemed of the Lord, shewe how he hath deliuered them from the hand of the oppressour,
Psalm 107:3	And gathered them out of the lands, from the East and from the West, from the North and from the South.
Psalm 107:4	When they wandered in the desert and wildernesse out of the waie, and founde no citie to dwell in,
Psalm 107:5	Both hungrie and thirstie, their soule fainted in them.
Psalm 107:6	Then they cried vnto the Lord in their trouble, and he deliuered them from their distresse,
Psalm 107:7	And led them forth by the right way, that they might goe to a citie of habitation.
Psalm 107:8	Let them therefore confesse before ye Lord his louing kindnesse, and his wonderfull woorkes before the sonnes of men.
Psalm 107:9	For he satisfied the thirstie soule, and filled the hungrie soule with goodnesse.
Psalm 107:10	They that dwell in darkenesse and in the shadowe of death, being bounde in miserie and yron,
Psalm 107:11	Because they rebelled against the wordes of the Lord, and despised the counsell of the most High,
Psalm 107:12	When he humbled their heart with heauines, then they fell downe and there was no helper.
Psalm 107:13	Then they cried vnto the Lord in their trouble, and he deliuered them from their distresse.
Psalm 107:14	He brought them out of darkenes, and out of the shadowe of death, and brake their bandes asunder.
Psalm 107:15	Let them therefore cofesse before the Lord his louing kindnesse, and his wonderfull woorkes before the sonnes of men.
Psalm 107:16	For hee hath broken the gates of brasse, and brast the barres of yron asunder.
Psalm 107:17	Fooles by reason of their transgression, and because of their iniquities are afflicted.
Psalm 107:18	Their soule abhorreth al meat, and they are brought to deaths doore.
Psalm 107:19	Then they crie vnto the Lord in their trouble, and he deliuereth them from their distresse.
Psalm 107:20	He sendeth his worde and healeth them, and deliuereth them from their graues.
Psalm 107:21	Let them therefore cofesse before the Lord his louing kindnesse, and his wonderful workes before the sonnes of men,
Psalm 107:22	And let them offer sacrifices of praise, and declare his workes with reioycing.
Psalm 107:23	They that goe downe to the sea in ships, and occupie by the great waters,
Psalm 107:24	They see the woorkes of the Lord, and his wonders in the deepe.
Psalm 107:25	For he commaundeth and raiseth the stormie winde, and it lifteth vp the waues thereof.
Psalm 107:26	They mount vp to the heauen, and descend to ye deepe, so that their soule melteth for trouble.
Psalm 107:27	They are tossed to and from, and stagger like a drunken man, and all their cunning is gone.
Psalm 107:28	Then they crie vnto the Lord in their trouble, and he bringeth them out of their distresse.
Psalm 107:29	He turneth the storme to calme, so that the waues thereof are still.
Psalm 107:30	When they are quieted, they are glad, and hee bringeth them vnto the hauen, where they would be.
Psalm 107:31	Let them therfore confesse before the Lord his louing kindnesse, and his wonderfull woorkes before the sonnes of men.
Psalm 107:32	And let them exalt him in the Congregation of the people, and praise him in the assembly of the Elders.
Psalm 107:33	He turneth the floodes into a wildernesse, and the springs of waters into drinesse,
Psalm 107:34	And a fruitfull land into barrennes for the wickednes of them that dwell therein.
Psalm 107:35	Againe hee turneth the wildernesse into pooles of water, and the drie lande into water springs.
Psalm 107:36	And there he placeth the hungrie, and they builde a citie to dwell in,
Psalm 107:37	And sowe the fieldes, and plant vineyardes, which bring foorth fruitfull increase.
Psalm 107:38	For he blesseth them, and they multiplie exceedingly, and he diminisheth not their cattell.
Psalm 107:39	Againe men are diminished, and brought lowe by oppression, euill and sorowe.
Psalm 107:40	He powreth contempt vpon princes, and causeth them to erre in desert places out of the way.
Psalm 107:41	Yet he raiseth vp the poore out of miserie, and maketh him families like a flocke of sheepe.
Psalm 107:42	The righteous shall see it, and reioyce, and all iniquitie shall stoppe her mouth.
Psalm 107:43	Who is wise that hee may obserue these things? for they shall vnderstand the louing kindnesse of the Lord.
Psalm 108:1	A song or Psalme of David. O God, mine heart is prepared, so is my tongue: I will sing and giue praise.
Psalm 108:2	Awake viole and harpe: I will awake early.
Psalm 108:3	I will praise thee, O Lord, among the people, and I wil sing vnto thee among the nations.
Psalm 108:4	For thy mercy is great aboue the heauens, and thy trueth vnto the clouds.
Psalm 108:5	Exalt thy self, O God, aboue the heauens, and let thy glorie be vpon all the earth,
Psalm 108:6	That thy beloued may be deliuered: helpe with thy right hand and heare me.
Psalm 108:7	God hath spoken in his holinesse: therefore I will reioyce, I shall deuide Shechem and measure the valley of Succoth.
Psalm 108:8	Gilead shalbe mine, and Manasseh shalbe mine: Ephraim also shalbe the strength of mine head: Iuda is my lawgiuer.
Psalm 108:9	Moab shalbe my washpot: ouer Edom wil I cast out my shoe: vpon Palestina wil I triumph.
Psalm 108:10	Who will leade mee into the strong citie? who will bring me vnto Edom?
Psalm 108:11	Wilt not thou, O God, which haddest forsaken vs, and diddest not goe foorth, O God, with our armies?
Psalm 108:12	Giue vs helpe against trouble: for vaine is the helpe of man.
Psalm 108:13	Through God we shall doe valiantly: for he shall treade downe our enemies.
Psalm 109:1	To him that excelleth. A Psalme of David. Holde not thy tongue, O God of my praise.
Psalm 109:2	For the mouth of the wicked, and the mouth full of deceite are opened vpon me: they haue spoken to me with a lying tongue.
Psalm 109:3	They compassed me about also with words of hatred, and fought against me without a cause.
Psalm 109:4	For my friendship they were mine aduersaries, but I gaue my selfe to praier.
Psalm 109:5	And they haue rewarded me euil for good, and hatred for my friendship.
Psalm 109:6	Set thou the wicked ouer him, and let the aduersarie stand at his right hand.
Psalm 109:7	Whe he shalbe iudged, let him be condemned, and let his praier be turned into sinne.
Psalm 109:8	Let his daies be fewe, and let another take his charge.
Psalm 109:9	Let his children be fatherlesse, and his wife a widowe.
Psalm 109:10	Let his children be vagabonds and beg and seeke bread, comming out of their places destroyed.
Psalm 109:11	Let the extortioner catch al that he hath, and let the strangers spoile his labour.
Psalm 109:12	Let there be none to extend mercie vnto him: neither let there be any to shewe mercie vpon his fatherlesse children.
Psalm 109:13	Let his posteritie be destroied, and in the generation following let their name be put out.
Psalm 109:14	Let the iniquitie of his fathers bee had in remembrance with the Lord: and let not the sinne of his mother be done away.
Psalm 109:15	But let them alway be before the Lord, that he may cut off their memorial from ye earth.
Psalm 109:16	Because he remembred not to shew mercie, but persecuted the afflicted and poore man, and the sorowfull hearted to slay him.
Psalm 109:17	As he loued cursing, so shall it come vnto him, and as he loued not blessing, so shall it be farre from him.
Psalm 109:18	As he clothed himselfe with cursing like a rayment, so shall it come into his bowels like water, and like oyle into his bones.
Psalm 109:19	Let it be vnto him as a garment to couer him, and for a girdle, wherewith he shalbe alway girded.
Psalm 109:20	Let this be the rewarde of mine aduersarie from the Lord, and of them, that speake euill against my soule.
Psalm 109:21	But thou, O Lord my God, deale with me according vnto thy Name: deliuer me, (for thy mercie is good)
Psalm 109:22	Because I am poore and needie, and mine heart is wounded within me.
Psalm 109:23	I depart like the shadowe that declineth, and am shaken off as the grashopper.
Psalm 109:24	My knees are weake through fasting, and my flesh hath lost all fatnes.
Psalm 109:25	I became also a rebuke vnto them: they that looked vpon me, shaked their heads.
Psalm 109:26	Helpe me, O Lord my God: saue me according to thy mercie.
Psalm 109:27	And they shall know, that this is thine hand, and that thou, Lord, hast done it.
Psalm 109:28	Though they curse, yet thou wilt blesse: they shall arise and be confounded, but thy seruant shall reioyce.
Psalm 109:29	Let mine aduersaries be clothed with shame, and let them couer themselues with their confusion, as with a cloke.
Psalm 109:30	I will giue thankes vnto the Lord greatly with my mouth and praise him among ye multitude.
Psalm 109:31	For he will stand at the right hand of the poore, to saue him from them that woulde condemne his soule.
Psalm 110:1	A Psalme of David. The Lord said vnto my Lord, Sit thou at my right hand, vntill I make thine enemies thy footestoole.
Psalm 110:2	The Lord shall send the rod of thy power out of Zion: be thou ruler in the middes of thine enemies.
Psalm 110:3	Thy people shall come willingly at the time of assembling thine armie in holy beautie: the youth of thy wombe shalbe as the morning dewe.
Psalm 110:4	The Lord sware and wil not repent, Thou art a Priest for euer after ye order of Melchi-zedek.
Psalm 110:5	The Lord, that is at thy right hand, shall wound Kings in the day of his wrath.
Psalm 110:6	He shalbe iudge among the heathen: he shall fill all with dead bodies, and smite the head ouer great countreis.
Psalm 110:7	He shall drinke of the brooke in the way: therefore shall he lift vp his head.
Psalm 111:1	Praise ye the Lord. I will prayse the Lord with my whole heart in the assemblie and Congregation of the iust.
Psalm 111:2	The workes of the Lord are great, and ought to be sought out of al them that loue them.
Psalm 111:3	His worke is beautifull and glorious, and his righteousnesse endureth for euer.
Psalm 111:4	He hath made his wonderfull workes to be had in remembrance: the Lord is mercifull and full of compassion.
Psalm 111:5	He hath giuen a portion vnto them that feare him: he wil euer be mindfull of his couenant.
Psalm 111:6	He hath shewed to his people the power of his workes in giuing vnto them the heritage of the heathen.
Psalm 111:7	The workes of his handes are trueth and iudgement: all his statutes are true.
Psalm 111:8	They are stablished for euer and euer, and are done in trueth and equitie.
Psalm 111:9	He sent redemption vnto his people: he hath commanded his couenant for euer: holy and fearefull is his Name.
Psalm 111:10	The beginning of wisedome is the feare of the Lord: all they that obserue them, haue good vnderstanding: his praise endureth for euer.
Psalm 112:1	Praise ye the Lord. Blessed is the man, that feareth the Lord, and deliteth greatly in his commandements.
Psalm 112:2	His seede shall be mightie vpon earth: the generation of the righteous shall be blessed.
Psalm 112:3	Riches and treasures shalbe in his house, and his righteousnesse endureth for euer.
Psalm 112:4	Vnto the righteous ariseth light in darkenes: he is merciful and full of copassion and righteous.
Psalm 112:5	A good man is mercifull and lendeth, and will measure his affaires by iudgement.
Psalm 112:6	Surely he shall neuer be moued: but the righteous shalbe had in euerlasting remembrance.
Psalm 112:7	He will not be afraide of euill tidings: for his heart is fixed, and beleeueth in the Lord.
Psalm 112:8	His heart is stablished: therefore he will not feare, vntill he see his desire vpon his enemies.
Psalm 112:9	He hath distributed and giuen to ye poore: his righteousnesse remaineth for euer: his horne shalbe exalted with glory.
Psalm 112:10	The wicked shall see it and be angrie: he shall gnash with his teeth, and consume away: the desire of the wicked shall perish.
Psalm 113:1	Praise ye the Lord. Praise, O ye seruants of the Lord, prayse the Name of the Lord.
Psalm 113:2	Blessed be the Name of the Lord from hencefoorth and for euer.
Psalm 113:3	The Lordes Name is praysed from the rising of ye sunne, vnto ye going downe of the same.
Psalm 113:4	The Lord is high aboue all nations, and his glorie aboue the heauens.
Psalm 113:5	Who is like vnto the Lord our God, that hath his dwelling on high!
Psalm 113:6	Who abaseth himselfe to beholde things in the heauen and in the earth!
Psalm 113:7	He raiseth the needie out of the dust, and lifteth vp the poore out of the dung,
Psalm 113:8	That he may set him with the princes, euen with the princes of his people.
Psalm 113:9	He maketh the barren woman to dwell with a familie, and a ioyfull mother of children. Prayse ye the Lord.
Psalm 114:1	When Israel went out of Egypt, and the house of Iaakob from the barbarous people,
Psalm 114:2	Iudah was his sanctification, and Israel his dominion.
Psalm 114:3	The Sea sawe it and fled: Iorden was turned backe.
Psalm 114:4	The mountaines leaped like rams, and the hils as lambes.
Psalm 114:5	What ailed thee, O Sea, that thou fleddest? O Iorden, why wast thou turned backe?
Psalm 114:6	Ye mountaines, why leaped ye like rams, and ye hils as lambes?
Psalm 114:7	The earth trembled at the presence of the Lord, at the presence of the God of Iaakob,
Psalm 114:8	Which turneth the rocke into waterpooles, and the flint into a fountaine of water.
Psalm 115:1	Not vnto vs, O Lord, not vnto vs, but vnto thy Name giue the glorie, for thy louing mercie and for thy truethes sake.
Psalm 115:2	Wherefore shall the heathen say, Where is nowe their God?
Psalm 115:3	But our God is in heauen: he doeth what so euer he will.
Psalm 115:4	Their idoles are siluer and golde, euen the worke of mens hands.
Psalm 115:5	They haue a mouth and speake not: they haue eyes and see not.
Psalm 115:6	They haue eares and heare not: they haue noses and smelll not.
Psalm 115:7	They haue handes and touche not: they haue feete and walke not: neither make they a sound with their throte.
Psalm 115:8	They that make them are like vnto them: so are all that trust in them.
Psalm 115:9	O Israel, trust thou in the Lord: for he is their helpe and their shielde.
Psalm 115:10	O house of Aaron, trust ye in the Lord: for he is their helpe and their shielde.
Psalm 115:11	Ye that feare the Lord, trust in the Lord: for he is their helper and their shield.
Psalm 115:12	The Lord hath bene mindfull of vs: he will blesse, he will blesse the house of Israel, he will blesse the house of Aaron.
Psalm 115:13	He will blesse them that feare the Lord, both small and great.
Psalm 115:14	The Lord will increase his graces towarde you, euen toward you and toward your children.
Psalm 115:15	Ye are blessed of the Lord, which made the heauen and the earth.
Psalm 115:16	The heauens, euen the heauens are the Lordes: but he hath giuen the earth to the sonnes of men.
Psalm 115:17	The dead prayse not the Lord, neither any that goe downe into the place of silence.
Psalm 115:18	But we will prayse the Lord from henceforth and for euer. Prayse ye the Lord.
Psalm 116:1	I love the Lord, because he hath heard my voyce and my prayers.
Psalm 116:2	For he hath inclined his eare vnto me, whe I did call vpon him in my dayes.
Psalm 116:3	When the snares of death copassed me, and the griefes of the graue caught me: when I founde trouble and sorowe.
Psalm 116:4	Then I called vpon the Name of the Lord, saying, I beseech thee, O Lord, deliuer my soule.
Psalm 116:5	The Lord is mercifull and righteous, and our God is full of compassion.
Psalm 116:6	The Lord preserueth the simple: I was in miserie and he saued me.
Psalm 116:7	Returne vnto thy rest, O my soule: for the Lord hath bene beneficiall vnto thee,
Psalm 116:8	Because thou hast deliuered my soule from death, mine eyes from teares, and my feete from falling.
Psalm 116:9	I shall walke before the Lord in the lande of the liuing.
Psalm 116:10	I beleeued, therefore did I speake: for I was sore troubled.
Psalm 116:11	I said in my feare, All men are lyers.
Psalm 116:12	What shall I render vnto the Lord for all his benefites toward me?
Psalm 116:13	I will take the cup of saluation, and call vpon the Name of the Lord.
Psalm 116:14	I will pay my vowes vnto the Lord, euen nowe in the presence of all his people.

Psalm 117:1	All nations, praise ye the Lord: all ye people, praise him.

Psalm 118:1	Praise yee the Lord, because he is good: for his mercie endureth for euer.
Psalm 118:2	Let Israel now say, That his mercy endureth for euer.
Psalm 118:3	Let the house of Aaron nowe say, That his mercy endureth for euer.
Psalm 118:4	Let them, that feare the Lord, nowe say, That his mercie endureth for euer.
Psalm 118:5	I called vpon the Lord in trouble, and the Lord heard me, and set me at large.
Psalm 118:6	The Lord is with mee: therefore I will not feare what man can doe vnto me.
Psalm 118:7	The Lord is with mee among them that helpe me: therefore shall I see my desire vpon mine enemies.
Psalm 118:8	It is better to trust in the Lord, then to haue confidence in man.
Psalm 118:9	It is better to trust in the Lord, then to haue confidence in princes.
Psalm 118:10	All nations haue compassed me: but in the Name of the Lord shall I destroy them.

Psalm 118:12	They came about mee like bees, but they were quenched as a fire of thornes: for in the Name of the Lord I shall destroy them.
Psalm 118:13	Thou hast thrust sore at me, that I might fall: but the Lord hath holpen me.
Psalm 118:14	The Lord is my strength and song: for he hath beene my deliuerance.

Psalm 118:17	I shall not die, but liue, and declare the woorkes of the Lord.
Psalm 118:18	The Lord hath chastened me sore, but he hath not deliuered me to death.
Psalm 118:19	Open ye vnto me the gates of righteousnes, that I may goe into them, and praise the Lord.
Psalm 118:20	This is the gate of the Lord: the righteous shall enter into it.
Psalm 118:21	I will praise thee: for thou hast heard mee, and hast beene my deliuerance.
Psalm 118:22	The stone, which the builders refused, is the head of the corner.
Psalm 118:23	This was the Lordes doing, and it is marueilous in our eyes.
Psalm 118:24	This is the day, which the Lord hath made: let vs reioyce and be glad in it.
